    46	sha(){ python3 -c "import p4_lib;print(p4_lib.sha256_file('$1'))" 2>/dev/null; }
    47	
    48	unfold_one(){
    49	  local BAND="$1" EP="$2" tag="${1}_${2}"
    50	  local MERGED="${MERGEDIR}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_${tag}.root"
    51	  local OUT="${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${tag}.root"
    52	  local REC="${OUT}.done"
    53	  # D2a: the skip is now CONTENT-validating. A ROOT plus any nonempty .done used to be enough;
    54	  # p4_check_receipt.py re-derives root/central/config/bkg_mode identities live and compares the
    55	  # merged sha against the orchestrator receipt. A reject falls through and re-runs the endpoint.
    56	  if [[ -s "${OUT}" && -s "${REC}" ]] && valid_root "${OUT}"; then
    57	    if RCHK=$(python3 p4_check_receipt.py --receipt "${REC}" --tag "${tag}" \
    58	                --root "${OUT}" --merged "${MERGED}" 2>&1); then
    59	      echo "[unfold] SKIP ${tag} (receipt validated)"; return 0
    60	    fi
    61	    echo "[unfold] STALE ${tag} -> re-running: ${RCHK}"
    62	    rm -f "${REC}"                       # D2: never leave a stale ROOT/receipt pair behind
    63	  fi
    64	  # REPAIR-6: the LEGACY-ATTEST path is DELETED, not repaired.
    65	  #
    66	  # It matched a legacy ROOT's sha256 against the committed manifest and then wrote a receipt
    67	  # stamped with the CURRENT code_rev and unfold_blob -- asserting that today's driver produced
    68	  # a file made on 2026-07-18 by an older one. That is a provenance lie, and guarding it would
    69	  # only have made the lie harder to see (repair-5 verifier finding 1, and the same class as
    70	  # KNOWN_ISSUES #23 and BEN-043's "a checkpoint is not provenance unless something asserts it
    71	  # reproduces the product").
    72	  #
    73	  # A second, independent reason it had to go: attestation-by-hash rested on the endpoint ROOTs
    74	  # being bit-reproducible, and they are NOT (KNOWN_ISSUES #24, measured 2026-08-07 -- 0/10
    75	  # sha256 match on a clean re-unfold while contents agreed to 1.9e-11 per bin and 2.6e-14 on
    76	  # the integral). So "re-unfold and compare hashes" could never have succeeded; the path could
    77	  # only ever certify that a file had not changed on disk, which is storage integrity, not
    78	  # provenance.
    79	  #
    80	  # The replacement is not a better guard -- it is removing the need for one. Every endpoint is
    81	  # PRODUCED by this launcher, so the receipt's producer claim is true by construction. The ten
    82	  # 2026-07-18 ROOTs are preserved under unfolds__SUPERSEDED_20260718/ and are never read here.
    83	  [[ ! -s "${MERGED}" ]] && { echo "[unfold] ABORT ${tag} merged missing"; return 3; }
    84	  local TMP="${OUT}.$$.${RANDOM}.tmp.root"
    85	  rm -f "${TMP}"
    86	  # `-u`: unbuffered. On this Lustre filesystem st_blksize is 4 MiB, so a buffered redirect
    87	  # shows ZERO progress for the whole run and liveness has to be inferred from sstat
    88	  # instead (BEN-028). Cost me an hour of blind watching on the 2026-08-07 probe run.
    89	  if python3 -u unfold_nd_omnifold_unbinned.py --omnifile "${MERGED}" --axes eavail,q3,W \
    90	       --iters 5 --use-weights --estimator lgbm --seed 42 --bkg-mode "${BKG_MODE}" \
    91	       --out "${TMP}" --verbose \
    92	       > "${OUTDIR}/unfold_${tag}.log" 2>&1 && valid_root "${TMP}"; then
    93	    local MH CH RH; MH=$(sha "${MERGED}"); CH=$(sha "products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root")
    94	    mv -f "${TMP}" "${OUT}"; RH=$(sha "${OUT}")                       # atomic ROOT publish
    95	    # D2c: this used to be an unchecked `printf … && mv`, so a failed receipt write still fell
    96	    # through to `echo DONE` and returned 0 -- a published ROOT with no receipt, reported as
    97	    # success. The write is now the function's success condition.
    98	    if ! { printf '{"tag":"%s","mode":"produced","root_sha256":"%s","merged_sha256":"%s","central5d_sha256":"%s","config_hash":"%s","bkg_mode":"%s","bkg_mode_basis":"passed explicitly to the driver by this launcher","code_rev":"%s","unfold_blob":"%s","t":"%s"}\n' \
    99	      "${tag}" "${RH}" "${MH}" "${CH}" "${CFG_HASH}" "${BKG_MODE}" "${CODE_REV}" "${UNFOLD_BLOB}" "$(date -u +%FT%TZ)" > "${REC}.tmp" && mv -f "${REC}.tmp" "${REC}"; }; then
   100	      echo "[unfold] FAIL ${tag} receipt publication failed after ROOT publish"; rm -f "${REC}.tmp"; return 8
   101	    fi
   102	    echo "[unfold] DONE ${tag}"
   103	  else
   104	    echo "[unfold] FAIL ${tag} (see unfold_${tag}.log)"; rm -f "${TMP}"; return 4
   105	  fi
   106	}
   107	
   108	declare -A RC
   109	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
   110	  while [ "$(jobs -rp | wc -l)" -ge "$CONC" ]; do sleep 8; done
   111	  ( unfold_one "${BAND}" "${EP}" ) & RC["${BAND}_${EP}"]=$!
   112	done; done
   113	fail=0
   114	for tag in "${!RC[@]}"; do wait "${RC[$tag]}" || { echo "[p4-unfold] worker FAILED: ${tag}"; fail=1; }; done
   115	
   116	# exact 10-tag inventory of published (ROOT + receipt) endpoints
   117	missing=0
   118	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
   119	  t="${BAND}_${EP}"; [[ -s "${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${t}.root" && -s "${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${t}.root.done" ]] || { echo "[p4-unfold] MISSING published ${t}"; missing=1; }
   120	done; done
   121	# D2d: the loop above only asks whether the ten EXPECTED tags exist, so an eleventh product in
   122	# the directory was invisible to it. Reject extras as well as missing -- an unexplained endpoint
   123	# in a publication namespace is exactly the state this lane spent three weeks in.
   124	extras=0
   125	LIVE_TAGS=$(ls "${OUTDIR}"/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_*.root 2>/dev/null \
   126	  | sed -e 's#.*uni_full_##' -e 's#\.root$##' | sort)
   127	python3 -c "
   128	import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
   129	import p4_lib as P
   130	tags=[t for t in sys.stdin.read().split() if t]
   131	P.require_exact_endpoint_tags(tags)
   132	" <<< "${LIVE_TAGS}" || { echo "[p4-unfold] EXTRA/UNEXPECTED endpoint products present"; extras=1; }
   133	if [[ "${fail}" -ne 0 || "${missing}" -ne 0 || "${extras}" -ne 0 ]]; then echo "[p4-unfold] FAIL-CLOSED (worker fail, incomplete inventory, or extra products)"; exit 5; fi
   134	echo "[p4-unfold] COMPLETE 10/10 published+receipted $(date -u +%T)"

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba 'nd-unfolding/p4_check_verifier_token.py'" in /Users/josephbailey/local-research/MINERvA-OmniFold/.claude/worktrees/gbdt-closeout
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Bind P4_VERIFIER_PASS to a real verifier verdict (closes KNOWN_ISSUES #21 / inventory A1).
     3	
     4	The gate at `run_p4_standard.sh:41` tested only `[[ -z "${P4_VERIFIER_PASS}" ]]`, so ANY
     5	non-empty string authorized covariance construction, and the refusal message named the variable
     6	to set. It was demonstrated load-bearing on 2026-08-07: an autonomous run that self-authorized
     7	would have written a candidate ROOT at stage 4 and died at stage 5, because stages 4-6 had never
     8	executed and were broken three independent ways.
     9	
    10	**The fix is deliberately not "check the variable harder."** An agent that can set an env var
    11	can also write a JSON file, so a token bound to a file the agent could create is no gate at all.
    12	The token must resolve to a verdict that is:
    13	
    14	  1. a file whose sha256 EQUALS the token                     (the token is not a password,
    15	                                                               it is the digest of the evidence)
    16	  2. TRACKED in git and identical to its committed blob        (so it cannot be fabricated in the
    17	                                                               working tree, and reviewing it is
    18	                                                               reviewing what authorized the run)
    19	  3. `verdict == "PASS"`                                       (BLOCK/absent authorizes nothing)
    20	  4a. `code_rev` is an ANCESTOR of HEAD                        (the verdict came from this
    21	                                                               history, not a foreign branch)
    22	  4b. every file the verdict REVIEWED is byte-identical        (a PASS cannot authorize code the
    23	      between that commit and HEAD                              verifier never saw)
    24	
    25	Rule 4 was originally `code_rev == HEAD`, which broke on correct behaviour: any push by another
    26	lane between the PASS and stages 4-6 invalidated a good verdict over commits touching nothing
    27	reviewed. 4a+4b is strictly stronger -- it checks what the rule protects instead of a proxy that
    28	unrelated commits perturb. Scope comes from the verdict's `review_scope` if declared, else the
    29	whole standard-P4 surface, which is the wider and safer default.
    30	
    31	That still does not make forgery impossible -- someone can commit a fake verdict -- but it moves
    32	the act from "set a variable nobody will ever see" to "commit a falsified receipt into the
    33	ledger under your own name," which is the difference between an accident and a decision.
    34	
    35	Usage:  p4_check_verifier_token.py --token <sha256>
    36	Exit 0 and print the resolved receipt path, or print TOKEN-REJECT :: <reason> and exit 1.
    37	"""
    38	import argparse, glob, json, os, subprocess, sys
    39	
    40	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    41	import p4_lib as P
    42	
    43	RECEIPT_DIR = os.path.join(P.REPO_ROOT, "docs", "orchestration", "runs", "standard-p4-verifier")
    44	
    45	
    46	def _git(*args):
    47	    return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True,
    48	                                   stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
    49	
    50	
    51	def resolve(token):
    52	    if not token or not token.strip():
    53	        raise P.P4GateError("empty token")
    54	    token = token.strip().lower()
    55	    if len(token) != 64 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in token):
    56	        raise P.P4GateError(
    57	            f"token is not a sha256 ({len(token)} chars). The token must be the digest of a "
    58	            f"committed standard-p4-verifier verdict, not a passphrase.")
    59	    if not os.path.isdir(RECEIPT_DIR):
    60	        raise P.P4GateError(f"no verifier receipt directory at {RECEIPT_DIR}")
    61	
    62	    head = _git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
    63	    matches = []
    64	    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(RECEIPT_DIR, "*.json"))):
    65	        if P.sha256_file(p) == token:
    66	            matches.append(p)
    67	    if not matches:
    68	        raise P.P4GateError(
    69	            f"no verdict in {os.path.relpath(RECEIPT_DIR, P.REPO_ROOT)} has sha256 {token[:16]}… "
    70	            f"-- the token must be the digest of an actual receipt")
    71	    if len(matches) > 1:
    72	        raise P.P4GateError(f"token matches {len(matches)} receipts; ambiguous")
    73	    path = matches[0]
    74	    rel = os.path.relpath(path, P.REPO_ROOT)
    75	
    76	    # (2) tracked AND unmodified -- a receipt invented in the working tree must not authorize
    77	    try:
    78	        _git("ls-files", "--error-unmatch", rel)
    79	    except Exception:
    80	        raise P.P4GateError(f"{rel} is not tracked in git; an untracked verdict authorizes nothing")
    81	    committed = _git("rev-parse", f"HEAD:{rel}")
    82	    worktree = _git("hash-object", rel)
    83	    if committed != worktree:
    84	        raise P.P4GateError(
    85	            f"{rel} differs from its committed blob (working {worktree[:12]} vs committed "
    86	            f"{committed[:12]}); the authorizing verdict must be the reviewed one")
    87	
    88	    v = json.load(open(path))
    89	    # (3) verdict
    90	    if str(v.get("verdict", "")).upper() != "PASS":
    91	        raise P.P4GateError(
    92	            f"{rel} records verdict={v.get('verdict')!r}, not PASS -- this authorizes nothing")
    93	    # (4) REPAIR-6c. This required `code_rev == HEAD` and was wrong for the same reason the
    94	    # receipt gate was: it breaks on CORRECT behaviour. Another lane pushing between the verifier
    95	    # PASS and stages 4-6 -- which happened today, eight commits mid-run -- would reject a valid
    96	    # verdict over commits touching nothing the verifier reviewed.
    97	    #
    98	    # Replaced by a pair that is STRICTLY STRONGER than equality, because it checks the thing the
    99	    # rule protects rather than a proxy unrelated commits perturb:
   100	    #   (4a) the reviewed commit must be an ANCESTOR of HEAD -- catches a verdict carried in from
   101	    #        a foreign branch or a rewritten history;
   102	    #   (4b) every file the verdict actually REVIEWED must be byte-identical between that commit
   103	    #        and HEAD -- so a PASS cannot authorize code the verifier never saw.
   104	    # The verdict may declare its own scope in `review_scope`; absent that, the fallback is every
   105	    # tracked file on the standard-P4 surface, the wider and therefore safer assumption.
   106	    cr = v.get("code_rev")
   107	    if not cr:
   108	        raise P.P4GateError(f"{rel} records no code_rev; cannot tell which patch it passed")
   109	    cr = str(cr).strip()
   110	    if not P.code_rev_in_history(cr):
   111	        raise P.P4GateError(
   112	            f"{rel} passed code_rev {cr[:12]}, which is not an ancestor of HEAD ({head[:12]}). "
   113	            f"That verdict did not come from this repository's history.")
   114	
   115	    scope = v.get("review_scope")
   116	    if isinstance(scope, list) and scope:
   117	        paths, scope_src = sorted(str(x) for x in scope), "declared review_scope"
   118	    else:
   119	        paths = P.tracked_files_matching(P.STANDARD_P4_SURFACE_GLOBS, rev=head)
   120	        scope_src = "default standard-P4 surface (%d tracked files)" % len(paths)
   121	    if not paths:
   122	        raise P.P4GateError("could not resolve a review scope; refusing to authorize blind")
   123	    ok, differing = P.paths_unchanged_between(cr, head, paths)
   124	    if not ok:
   125	        raise P.P4GateError(
   126	            f"{rel} reviewed {cr[:12]}, but {len(differing)} file(s) it covered have CHANGED at "
   127	            f"HEAD ({head[:12]}): {', '.join(differing[:4])}"
   128	            f"{' ...' if len(differing) > 4 else ''}. A PASS cannot authorize code the verifier "
   129	            f"never saw; re-run the verifier.")
   130	    v["_resolved_review_scope"] = scope_src
   131	    if v.get("authorizes_covariance_stages_4_6") is False:
   132	        raise P.P4GateError(f"{rel} explicitly sets authorizes_covariance_stages_4_6=false")
   133	    return rel, v
   134	
   135	
   136	def main():
   137	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Endpoint-receipt gate for run_p4_unfold_std.sh's resume path (repair-4, verifier defect 2).
     3	
     4	Exit 0 iff the receipt is complete and every recorded identity matches the live/committed one;
     5	otherwise print `RECEIPT-REJECT :: <reason>` and exit 1, which makes the launcher re-run the
     6	endpoint transactionally instead of skipping it.
     7	
     8	Deliberately a separate CLI rather than inline `python3 -c` in the launcher: the previous skip
     9	logic was an unreadable one-liner, and a gate that cannot be unit-tested is the defect this
    10	whole repair round exists to remove. ROOT-free -- content validity of the ROOT itself is the
    11	launcher's `valid_root`, this is the provenance half.
    12	
    13	Usage:
    14	  p4_check_receipt.py --receipt R.done --tag BAND_EP --root OUT.root --merged MERGED.root
    15	"""
    16	import argparse, json, os, sys
    17	
    18	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    19	import p4_lib as P
    20	
    21	REPO = P.REPO_ROOT
    22	ND = P.ND_ROOT
    23	CEN5 = f"{ND}/products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root"
    24	RECDIR = f"{REPO}/docs/orchestration/state/merged-input-hashes/p4-merged-20260718"
    25	UNFOLD_SRC = "nd-unfolding/unfold_nd_omnifold_unbinned.py"
    26	
    27	
    28	def committed_unfold_blob():
    29	    """The unfold driver's blob AS COMMITTED at HEAD (repair-5, defect 2).
    30	
    31	    The receipt gate previously required `code_rev` to be a non-empty string and compared it to
    32	    nothing, and recorded no source identity at all -- so an endpoint produced under a changed
    33	    driver was still skipped. Both are now compared, and this is the value they compare against.
    34	    Fails closed (returns None -> caller rejects) rather than guessing."""
    35	    import subprocess
    36	    try:
    37	        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"HEAD:{UNFOLD_SRC}"],
    38	                                       cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
    39	    except Exception:
    40	        return None
    41	
    42	
    43	def current_code_rev():
    44	    import subprocess
    45	    try:
    46	        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
    47	                                       cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
    48	    except Exception:
    49	        return None
    50	
    51	
    52	def committed_merged_sha(merged_path):
    53	    """The orchestrator receipt's committed hash for this merged file, after its own live
    54	    size+integer-mtime check. Never re-hashes 53.8 GB."""
    55	    live = {}
    56	    for b in P.BANDS:
    57	        for ep in P.ENDPOINTS:
    58	            rel = (f"nd-unfolding/active_universe_5d/standard/merged/"
    59	                   f"runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_{b}_{ep}.root")
    60	            ap = f"{REPO}/{rel}"
    61	            st = os.stat(ap)
    62	            live[rel] = (st.st_size, int(st.st_mtime))
    63	    rec = P.validate_orchestrator_merged_receipt(RECDIR, live)
    64	    base = os.path.basename(merged_path)
    65	    for p, h in rec["merged_sha256"].items():
    66	        if os.path.basename(p) == base:
    67	            return h
    68	    raise P.P4GateError(f"{base} not in the orchestrator merged receipt")
    69	
    70	
    71	def main():
    72	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    73	    ap.add_argument("--receipt", required=True)
    74	    ap.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
    75	    ap.add_argument("--root", required=True)
    76	    ap.add_argument("--merged", required=True)
    77	    a = ap.parse_args()
    78	    try:
    79	        if not os.path.exists(a.receipt) or os.path.getsize(a.receipt) == 0:
    80	            raise P.P4GateError("receipt absent or empty")
    81	        try:
    82	            rec = json.load(open(a.receipt))
    83	        except Exception as e:
    84	            raise P.P4GateError(f"receipt is not valid JSON ({e})")
    85	        cfg = P.P4Config(); cfg.validate()
    86	        blob, rev = committed_unfold_blob(), current_code_rev()
    87	        if not blob:
    88	            raise P.P4GateError(f"cannot resolve the committed blob of {UNFOLD_SRC}; refusing to "
    89	                                f"accept a receipt whose producing source cannot be checked")
    90	        if not rev:
    91	            raise P.P4GateError("cannot resolve HEAD; refusing to accept a receipt whose "
    92	                                "code_rev cannot be compared")
    93	        P.validate_endpoint_receipt(
    94	            rec, tag=a.tag,
    95	            root_sha256=P.sha256_file(a.root),
    96	            merged_sha256=committed_merged_sha(a.merged),
    97	            central5d_sha256=P.sha256_file(CEN5),
    98	            config_hash=cfg.hash(),
    99	            bkg_mode=cfg.bkg_mode,
   100	            code_rev=rev,
   101	            unfold_blob=blob)
   102	    except P.P4GateError as e:
   103	        print(f"RECEIPT-REJECT :: {e}"); sys.exit(1)
   104	    except Exception as e:                      # never let an unexpected error read as PASS
   105	        print(f"RECEIPT-REJECT :: unexpected {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(1)
   106	    print(f"RECEIPT-OK :: {a.tag}")
   107	    sys.exit(0)
   108	
   109	
   110	if __name__ == "__main__":
   111	    main()

    67	# ------------------------------------------- reproducibility tolerance (repair-6, 2026-08-07)
    68	# These endpoint ROOTs are NOT bit-reproducible. Re-unfolding the same inputs with the same
    69	# code and the same seed gives different bytes, because LightGBM/OpenMP reduction order depends
    70	# on the thread partitioning and five OmniFold iterations amplify last-bit differences. So a
    71	# reproducibility gate here must compare CONTENTS at a declared tolerance; sha256 identity
    72	# cannot express "same computation" (KNOWN_ISSUES #24).
    73	#
    74	# DECLARED tolerance -- a SPECIFICATION set by Joseph 2026-08-07, deliberately conservative,
    75	# roughly two orders above the measured floor so a CONC change does not force a re-derivation.
    76	# This is NOT a tolerance loosened to make something failing pass: nothing was failing, and the
    77	# floor below was measured on a clean 10/10 run with zero failures.
    78	REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN = 1e-9        # per reported bin, relative
    79	REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL = 1e-11      # on the integrated cross section, relative
    80	# WIDENED 1e-12 -> 1e-11 by Joseph, 2026-08-08, from measured SPREAD. The per-bin leg carried 52x
    81	# margin (1.93e-11 vs 1e-9) while the integral leg carried 3.2x (3.14e-13 vs 1e-12), and the two
    82	# integral observations span 12x (2.6e-14, 3.14e-13) -- a leg set up to false-alarm on a third
    83	# run. At 1e-11 both legs carry comparable margin (32x and 52x) and the discrimination survives:
    84	# a uniform shift still passes per-bin and fails integral anywhere from 1e-11 to 1e-9, a 100x
    85	# window instead of 1000x.
    86	#
    87	# This is SPECIFICATION from measured spread with nothing currently failing -- not a tolerance
    88	# loosened to rescue a result. The alternative considered and REJECTED was keeping 1e-12 with a
    89	# documented escape clause for marginal breaches: a gate you can talk your way past is the exact
    90	# anti-pattern this lane spent three rounds removing. REPRO_MEASURED_FLOOR below stays at the
    91	# OBSERVED values so the specification and the measurement can never be conflated.
    92	
    93	# MEASURED floor, recorded separately from the declared tolerance on purpose: re-measuring it
    94	# must never silently move the gate. Job 56471429 (CONC=6) vs the 2026-07-18 set (CONC=4),
    95	# ten endpoints, 65856 bins each, 106940 reported bins pooled:
    96	#   worst relative bin difference   1.9e-11
    97	#   integral agreement              2.6e-14
    98	#   pooled mean deviation          -1.76e-13   (sd 1.96e-12)
    99	#   fraction of bins deviating +    0.4594     -- 26.6 sigma from 0.5, i.e. a REAL systematic
   100	#                                                 sign preference, which is the expected
   101	#                                                 signature of a deterministic rounding path
   102	#                                                 and NOT evidence of symmetric noise.
   103	# sqrt(N)*eps for ~1e7 events is 7.0e-13, the same order as the pooled mean.
   104	REPRO_MEASURED_FLOOR = {
   105	    "job": "56471429", "conc_new": 6, "conc_reference": 4,
   106	    "worst_rel_bin": 1.9e-11, "integral_rel": 2.6e-14,
   107	    "pooled_mean": -1.76e-13, "pooled_sd": 1.96e-12,
   108	    "frac_positive": 0.4594, "frac_positive_sigma_from_half": 26.6,
   109	    "n_reported_bins_pooled": 106940,
   110	}
   111	
   112	
   113	def check_reproducibility(a, b, rtol_bin=REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN, rtol_int=REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL):
   114	    """Compare two runs of the same endpoint by CONTENT, not by bytes.
   115	
   116	    Returns the measured (max per-bin relative difference, integral relative difference).
   117	    Fails closed if either exceeds the declared tolerance."""
   118	    a = np.asarray(a, float); b = np.asarray(b, float)
   119	    require(a.shape == b.shape, f"reproducibility: shape {a.shape} != {b.shape}")
   120	    m = np.abs(b) > 0
   121	    require(m.any(), "reproducibility: reference has no positive bins")
   122	    rel = np.max(np.abs(a[m] - b[m]) / np.abs(b[m]))
   123	    sa, sb = float(a.sum()), float(b.sum())
   124	    rel_int = abs(sa / sb - 1.0) if sb != 0 else float("inf")
   125	    require(rel <= rtol_bin,
   126	            f"reproducibility: max per-bin relative difference {rel:.3e} > {rtol_bin:.0e}")
   127	    require(rel_int <= rtol_int,
   128	            f"reproducibility: integral relative difference {rel_int:.3e} > {rtol_int:.0e}")
   129	    return {"max_rel_bin": float(rel), "rel_integral": float(rel_int)}

   146	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- config / hashes
   147	class P4Config:
   148	    """Frozen unfold configuration; its hash pins seed/axes/iters/estimator so a
   149	    covariance can never be built from mismatched-config endpoints."""
   150	    def __init__(self, axes="eavail,q3,W", iters=5, seed=42, estimator="lgbm",
   151	                 use_weights=True, universe=None, bkg_mode=STANDARD_BKG_MODE):
   152	        self.axes = axes; self.iters = int(iters); self.seed = int(seed)
   153	        self.estimator = estimator; self.use_weights = bool(use_weights)
   154	        self.universe = universe          # MUST be None for active endpoints
   155	        self.bkg_mode = bkg_mode          # G-1: never inherited from the driver default
   156	    def as_dict(self):
   157	        # repair-4 (D3a): `full_phase_space_reported_grid` used to be bolted onto
   158	        # man["config"] in p4_evidence.py AFTER config_hash was computed, so the recorded hash
   159	        # did not cover the recorded configuration -- the declared config was not hash-bound.
   160	        # It belongs in the config object, where as_dict() and hash() both see it.
   161	        return {"axes": self.axes, "iters": self.iters, "seed": self.seed,
   162	                "estimator": self.estimator, "use_weights": self.use_weights,
   163	                "universe": self.universe, "bkg_mode": self.bkg_mode,
   164	                "full_phase_space_reported_grid": GRID_NBINS}
   165	    def hash(self):
   166	        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(self.as_dict(), sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
   167	    def footing(self):
   168	        """The nested footing block as the PRODUCER emits it. Consumers must read this
   169	        shape, not a flattened copy of it -- a fixture shaped like the consumer is how
   170	        BEN-040's gate stayed green while failing on every real input."""
   171	        f = dict(STANDARD_REQUIRED_FOOTING)
   172	        f.update({"estimator": self.estimator, "seed": self.seed, "iters": self.iters,
   173	                  "use_weights": self.use_weights, "bkg_mode": self.bkg_mode})
   174	        return f
   175	    def validate(self):
   176	        require(self.universe is None, "active endpoint config must not set --universe")
   177	        require(self.seed == 42, f"standard P4 requires fixed seed 42 (got {self.seed})")
   178	        require(self.use_weights, "standard P4 requires --use-weights")
   179	        require(self.iters == 5, f"standard P4 production uses 5 iters (got {self.iters})")
   180	        require(self.axes == "eavail,q3,W", f"standard P4 requires axes=eavail,q3,W (got {self.axes})")
   181	        require(self.estimator == "lgbm", f"standard P4 requires estimator=lgbm (got {self.estimator})")
   182	        require(self.bkg_mode in KNOWN_BKG_MODES,
   183	                f"unknown bkg_mode {self.bkg_mode!r} (known: {list(KNOWN_BKG_MODES)})")

   187	                f"and invalidates the J28-corrected covariance")
   188	        return True

   206	def classify_log_bkg_mode(text):
   207	    """Return (mode, reason) for one unfold log. mode is a member of KNOWN_BKG_MODES, or
   208	    None when the log cannot decide -- which callers must treat as UNPROVABLE, not as
   209	    'probably fine'."""
   210	    announced = None
   211	    for line in text.splitlines():
   212	        i = line.find(_BKG_MODE_ANNOUNCE)
   213	        if i >= 0:

   215	            tok = tail.split(":", 1)[0].strip()
   216	            if tok in KNOWN_BKG_MODES:
   217	                if announced is not None and announced != tok:
   218	                    return None, f"log announces conflicting modes {announced!r} and {tok!r}"
   219	                announced = tok
   220	            else:

   221	                return None, f"log announces unrecognized bkg-mode {tok!r}"

   225	            # log did not come from this driver's known print set.
   226	            return None, "log announces bkg-mode=purity, which this driver's purity branch never prints"
   227	        return announced, f"driver announced bkg-mode={announced}"
   228	    if _PURITY_SIGNATURE in text:
   229	        return "purity", ("no bkg-mode announcement + build_measured_training_nd signature "
   230	                          "present => purity branch (the silent branch)")
   231	    return None, ("no bkg-mode announcement and no measured-training signature; the run may "
   232	                  "have been non-verbose, so the branch is unprovable from this log")

   235	def require_standard_footing(manifest, required_bkg_mode=STANDARD_BKG_MODE):
   236	    """Fail closed unless the manifest carries a complete footing block that matches the
   237	    standard requirement. Mirrors fps_provenance.require_footing's semantics -- absent is
   238	    'unprovable' and fails, exactly like mismatched -- without importing or mutating it."""
   239	    foot = manifest.get("footing")
   240	    require(isinstance(foot, dict) and foot,
   241	            "manifest has no footing block (unprovable): the standard lane must record its "
   242	            "background footing, not inherit the driver default")
   243	    for k, v in STANDARD_REQUIRED_FOOTING.items():
   244	        require(foot.get(k) == v, f"footing.{k}={foot.get(k)!r} != {v!r}")
   245	    require("bkg_mode" in foot, "footing has no bkg_mode (unprovable)")
   246	    require(foot["bkg_mode"] in KNOWN_BKG_MODES,
   247	            f"footing.bkg_mode={foot['bkg_mode']!r} is not a known mode")
   248	    if required_bkg_mode is not None:
   249	        require(foot["bkg_mode"] == required_bkg_mode,
   250	                f"footing.bkg_mode={foot['bkg_mode']!r} != required {required_bkg_mode!r}")
   251	    return True

   261	_ADOPTED_TOKENS = ("uq_universe_5d_covariance_combined", "_uthrow", "_cvcentered",
   262	                   "adopted", "uq_4d/corrected", "universe_stage2_5d",
   263	                   "products/5d/xsec", "products/4d/xsec")
   264	
   265	
   266	def require_candidate_path(path):
   267	    """Positive allowlist + negative denylist: a candidate MUST live under the
   268	    candidate subdir and MUST NOT match any adopted/protected token. Prevents both
   269	    the round-2 self-rejection (candidate name containing '_final') and any write
   270	    onto an adopted/central path."""
   271	    # repair-5 (defect 4a). History: this was first a bare substring test, then a
   272	    # normpath component match -- and the verifier defeated the second one too, because a
   273	    # component match succeeds ANYWHERE the sequence appears, so
   274	    # `/evil/active_universe_5d/standard/candidate/out.root` passed, and normpath does not
   275	    # resolve symlinks. Containment must be RESOLVED and anchored to this repository:
   276	    # realpath both sides, then require commonpath(candidate_root, target) == candidate_root.
   277	    cand_root = os.path.realpath(os.path.join(ND_ROOT, CANDIDATE_SUBDIR))
   278	    # Callers pass repo-relative ("nd-unfolding/active_universe_5d/...") or ND-relative
   279	    # ("active_universe_5d/...") paths depending on where they cd to, so resolve a relative
   280	    # path against BOTH known roots and accept it if either lands inside. An absolute path is
   281	    # taken as given. Resolution is realpath, so symlinks cannot smuggle a target out.
   282	    if os.path.isabs(path):
   283	        cands = [path]
   284	    else:
   285	        cands = [os.path.join(ND_ROOT, path), os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, path)]
   286	    resolved, inside = None, False
   287	    for c in cands:
   288	        t = os.path.realpath(c)
   289	        if resolved is None:
   290	            resolved = t
   291	        try:
   292	            if os.path.commonpath([cand_root, t]) == cand_root and t != cand_root:
   293	                resolved, inside = t, True
   294	                break
   295	        except ValueError:                 # different drives / unrelated roots
   296	            continue
   297	    require(inside,
   298	            f"candidate must resolve inside {cand_root} (got {path} -> {resolved})")
   299	    for t in _ADOPTED_TOKENS:
   300	        require(t not in path, f"refusing candidate onto adopted/protected path (token {t!r})")
   301	    return True
   302	
   303	
   304	def prove_identity(A, B, rtol, label):
   305	    """Fail-closed max-relative-difference identity gate (no subtraction hidden)."""
   306	    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float); B = np.asarray(B, dtype=float)
   307	    require(A.shape == B.shape, f"{label}: shape {A.shape} != {B.shape}")
   308	    denom = max(1e-300, float(np.max(np.abs(A))))
   309	    err = float(np.max(np.abs(A - B)) / denom)
   310	    require(err <= rtol, f"{label}: identity broken (rel {err:.2e} > {rtol:.0e})")
   311	    return err
   312	
   313	
   314	def edges_bin_volume_hash(edges):
   315	    """Hash the ordered 5-axis edge arrays + the C-order per-bin volume vector."""
   316	    import numpy as _np
   317	    parts = []
   318	    for e in edges:
   319	        a = _np.asarray(e, dtype=float); require(a.ndim == 1 and a.size >= 2, "bad edge array")
   320	        parts.append(a.tobytes())
   321	    edge_hash = hashlib.sha256(b"|".join(parts)).hexdigest()
   322	    widths = [(_np.asarray(e, float)[1:] - _np.asarray(e, float)[:-1]) for e in edges]
   323	    vol = widths[0]
   324	    for w in widths[1:]:
   325	        vol = _np.multiply.outer(vol, w).ravel()   # C-order product of bin widths
   326	    vol_hash = hashlib.sha256(vol.astype(float).tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()
   327	    return {"edge_hash": edge_hash, "bin_volume_hash": vol_hash, "n_bins": int(vol.size)}
   328	
   329	
   330	def validate_orchestrator_merged_receipt(recdir, live_stat):
   331	    """Consume the owner-neutral merged-hash receipt: require COMPLETE + the four
   332	    files, recompute live size/integer-mtime vs the inventory, and return the
   333	    committed hash-list digest bound into our manifest. live_stat: dict path->(size,mtime)."""
   334	    import os as _os
   335	    for f in ("COMPLETE", "summary.tsv", "validation.tsv", "standard.sha256", "standard.inventory.tsv"):
   336	        require(_os.path.exists(_os.path.join(recdir, f)), f"orchestrator receipt missing {f}")
   337	    sha_lines = [l for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.sha256")).read().splitlines() if l.strip()]
   338	    require(len(sha_lines) == N_ENDPOINTS, f"receipt standard.sha256 has {len(sha_lines)} != 10 lines")
   339	    merged = {}
   340	    for l in sha_lines:
   341	        h, p = l.split(None, 1); merged[p.strip()] = h
   342	    # recompute live size + integer mtime against the committed inventory
   343	    inv = {}
   344	    for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.inventory.tsv")).read().splitlines():
   345	        if not l.strip() or l.startswith("#"):
   346	            continue
   347	        cols = l.split("\t")
   348	        if len(cols) >= 3:                       # orchestrator format: size<TAB>mtime<TAB>path
   349	            inv[cols[2]] = (int(cols[0]), int(float(cols[1])))
   350	    for p, (sz, mt) in inv.items():
   351	        require(p in live_stat, f"inventory path not live: {p}")
   352	        lsz, lmt = live_stat[p]
   353	        require(lsz == sz and int(lmt) == mt, f"live size/mtime drift for {p}")
   354	    digest = hashlib.sha256("\n".join(sorted(f"{merged[p]}  {p}" for p in merged)).encode()).hexdigest()
   355	    return {"merged_sha256": merged, "hash_list_digest": digest, "n": len(merged)}
   356	
   357	
   358	# ------------------------------------------- endpoint receipt schema (repair-4, defect 2)
   359	# Before repair-4 the resume path skipped an endpoint on `[[ -s ROOT && -s RECEIPT ]]` plus a
   360	# ROOT-key/dimension check. Receipt tag, ROOT sha, merged sha, central sha, config hash and
   361	# source provenance were never read, and the legacy-attest receipt did not even record the
   362	# merged/central hashes -- so "resumable" meant "any nonempty file pair is accepted forever".
   363	# That is the same size-as-completion family as BEN-023 and KNOWN_ISSUES #20(c).
   364	#
   365	# NOTE ON COST: the merged inputs are 53.8 GB each (538 GB total). This validator therefore
   366	# does NOT re-hash them; it compares the receipt's recorded merged sha against the
   367	# owner-neutral orchestrator receipt's committed hash, whose own live size+integer-mtime check
   368	# is what detects a changed input. Same reuse p4_evidence.py already relies on. The ROOT and
   369	# central files are ~480 KB and ARE re-hashed live on every skip.
   370	RECEIPT_MODES = ("produced", "legacy-attested")
   371	RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS = ("tag", "mode", "root_sha256", "merged_sha256", "central5d_sha256",
   372	                         "config_hash", "bkg_mode", "code_rev", "unfold_blob", "t")
   373	# The source whose blob decides whether a cached endpoint is still valid. The unfold driver is
   374	# the one that actually produces the ROOT; the others in p4_evidence.SRC describe downstream
   375	# consumers and do not invalidate an endpoint.
   376	RECEIPT_SOURCE_KEY = "unfold_blob"
   377	
   378	
   379	# The standard-P4 review surface: what a verifier verdict is understood to have reviewed when
   380	# it does not declare its own scope. Used by the token gate to prove that the files the verdict
   381	# covered have not changed between the reviewed commit and HEAD.
   382	STANDARD_P4_SURFACE_GLOBS = (
   383	    "nd-unfolding/p4_*.py",
   384	    "nd-unfolding/run_p4_*.sh",
   385	    "nd-unfolding/tests/test_p4_*.py",
   386	)

   388	
   389	def tracked_files_matching(globs, rev="HEAD"):
   390	    """Tracked paths at `rev` matching any glob. Sorted, so the result is order-stable."""
   391	    out = set()
   392	    for g in globs:
   393	        try:
   394	            r = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", rev],
   395	                                        cwd=REPO_ROOT, text=True,
   396	                                        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).splitlines()
   397	        except Exception:
   398	            return []
   399	        import fnmatch
   400	        out.update(p for p in r if fnmatch.fnmatch(p, g))
   401	    return sorted(out)
   402	
   403	
   404	def paths_unchanged_between(rev_a, rev_b, paths):
   405	    """Every path in `paths` has the same blob at rev_a and rev_b.
   406	
   407	    Returns (ok, [differing paths]). Fails CLOSED: if git cannot answer for a path, that path
   408	    counts as differing, because an unverifiable claim is not a satisfied one."""
   409	    differing = []
   410	    for p in paths:
   411	        try:
   412	            a = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{rev_a}:{p}"], cwd=REPO_ROOT,
   413	                                        text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   414	        except Exception:
   415	            a = None
   416	        try:
   417	            b = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{rev_b}:{p}"], cwd=REPO_ROOT,
   418	                                        text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   419	        except Exception:
   420	            b = None
   421	        if a is None or b is None or a != b:
   422	            differing.append(p)
   423	    return (not differing), differing
   424	
   425	
   426	def code_rev_in_history(rev, head=None):
   427	    """True iff `rev` is reachable from HEAD in this repository (ancestor of, or equal to).
   428	
   429	    Deliberately reachability rather than equality -- see the note in validate_endpoint_receipt.
   430	    Fails CLOSED: if git cannot answer, the answer is False, because an unverifiable provenance
   431	    claim is not a satisfied one."""
   432	    if not isinstance(rev, str) or not rev.strip():
   433	        return False
   434	    try:
   435	        subprocess.check_call(["git", "merge-base", "--is-ancestor", rev.strip(), head or "HEAD"],
   436	                              cwd=REPO_ROOT, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   437	        return True
   438	    except Exception:
   439	        return False

   442	def validate_endpoint_receipt(rec, *, tag, root_sha256, merged_sha256,
   443	                              central5d_sha256, config_hash, bkg_mode,
   444	                              code_rev, unfold_blob):
   445	    """Fail closed unless the receipt is complete AND every recorded identity matches the
   446	    live/committed one. Returns True, or raises P4GateError naming the first mismatch.
   447	
   448	    REPAIR-5 (defect 2). The previous version required `code_rev` to be a NON-EMPTY STRING and
   449	    compared it to nothing, and the receipt recorded no source identity at all -- so an
   450	    endpoint produced under a changed unfold driver was still skipped. That is the third
   451	    instance in this chain of "assert presence, never compare": the first is the
   452	    `P4_VERIFIER_PASS` non-emptiness gate (KNOWN_ISSUES #21), the second is the `identities`
   453	    block whose members were literal `True`. Presence is not evidence; both `code_rev` and the
   454	    producing driver's committed blob are now COMPARED."""
   455	    require(isinstance(rec, dict) and rec, f"receipt for {tag} is not a JSON object")
   456	    missing = [k for k in RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS if k not in rec]
   457	    require(not missing, f"receipt {tag} missing required keys {missing} (incomplete legacy format)")
   458	    require(rec["mode"] in RECEIPT_MODES, f"receipt {tag} unknown mode {rec['mode']!r}")
   459	    require(rec["tag"] == tag, f"receipt tag {rec['tag']!r} != {tag!r} (receipt/ROOT pair mismatch)")
   460	    require(rec["root_sha256"] == root_sha256,
   461	            f"receipt {tag} root_sha256 drift: recorded {rec['root_sha256']} vs live {root_sha256}")
   462	    require(rec["merged_sha256"] == merged_sha256,
   463	            f"receipt {tag} merged_sha256 drift vs the orchestrator receipt")
   464	    require(rec["central5d_sha256"] == central5d_sha256,
   465	            f"receipt {tag} central5d_sha256 drift (the central moved under this endpoint)")
   466	    require(rec["config_hash"] == config_hash,
   467	            f"receipt {tag} config_hash drift: produced under a different unfold configuration")
   468	    require(rec["bkg_mode"] == bkg_mode,
   469	            f"receipt {tag} bkg_mode {rec['bkg_mode']!r} != declared {bkg_mode!r}")
   470	    # D2: compared, not merely present.
   471	    require(isinstance(rec["code_rev"], str) and rec["code_rev"],
   472	            f"receipt {tag} has no code_rev")
   473	    require(isinstance(code_rev, str) and code_rev,
   474	            f"cannot validate receipt {tag}: no live code_rev to compare against")
   475	    # REPAIR-6b. This used to require code_rev == HEAD, and that was WRONG in the same way
   476	    # KNOWN_ISSUES #24 describes: it broke on correct behaviour. Any commit anywhere in the repo
   477	    # -- including another lane's, touching nothing this chain reads -- expired all ten receipts
   478	    # while the code that produced them was byte-identical. Caught by the self-check on the first
   479	    # production run: the PET lane advanced main by 8 commits mid-run, HEAD moved
   480	    # 42268b6 -> 203ff01, `git diff` on the unfold driver was EMPTY, and the gate rejected all ten.
   481	    #
   482	    # The producing-code binding is `unfold_blob`, which IS strictly compared below. `code_rev` is
   483	    # repository context, so the honest check is REACHABILITY, not equality: the receipt must come
   484	    # from this history. That still catches a receipt carried in from a foreign branch or a
   485	    # rewritten history, and does not expire on unrelated work.
   486	    require(code_rev_in_history(rec["code_rev"]),
   487	            f"receipt {tag} code_rev {rec['code_rev']} is not an ancestor of HEAD ({code_rev}): "
   488	            f"this receipt did not come from this repository's history")
   489	    require(isinstance(unfold_blob, str) and unfold_blob,
   490	            f"cannot validate receipt {tag}: no committed unfold-driver blob to compare against")
   491	    require(rec[RECEIPT_SOURCE_KEY] == unfold_blob,
   492	            f"receipt {tag} unfold_blob {rec[RECEIPT_SOURCE_KEY]} != committed {unfold_blob}: the "
   493	            f"unfold driver changed since this endpoint was produced")
   494	    return True

   497	def cmask_order_hash_4d(mask):
   498	    """4D reported-mask + C-order fingerprint (repair-4, defect 5c).
   499	
   500	    `mask_order_hash` fails closed on GRID_NBINS, which is the 5D grid, so the 4D mask had no
   501	    hash helper and the projector compared only the reported COUNT. A count is not an ordering:
   502	    a permuted 4D mask with the same population passed. Same construction as the 5D helper --
   503	    C-order indices of the selected bins -- so the two are comparable by eye in a manifest.
   504	    Deliberately NOT size-pinned: the 4D grid is a marginal of the 5D one and the standard lane
   505	    has no pinned canonical 4D target (see RUNBOOK-20260807 §7.4)."""
   506	    idx = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask))[0].astype(np.int64)
   507	    require(idx.size > 0, "4D reported mask is empty")
   508	    return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()

   511	def matrix_content_hash(M):
   512	    """Deterministic digest of a matrix's CONTENTS (repair-4, defect 5d).
   513	
   514	    The projection receipt recorded only `M_shape`, which two different projectors of the same
   515	    dimensions share. Hashes C-contiguous float64 bytes plus the shape, so a changed weight is
   516	    caught and a transposed/reshaped M cannot collide with the original."""
   517	    A = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(M, dtype=np.float64))
   518	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   519	    h.update(repr(A.shape).encode()); h.update(b"|C|f8|"); h.update(A.tobytes())
   520	    return h.hexdigest()

   523	def check_full_total_identity(Ccomb, Csyst, Cstat, Cml, rtol=1e-9):
   524	    """PROVE C_combined = C_syst + C_stat + C_ML by comparing the residual to the actual
   525	    stat and ML blocks. Returns the measured max-relative error.
   526	
   527	    REPAIR-5 (defect 4b / 6). Repair-4 checked only that `C_combined - C_syst` was symmetric
   528	    PSD and called that the full-total identity, in a gate name, a receipt field and a test
   529	    name. PSD is NECESSARY but nowhere near SUFFICIENT: every covariance-shaped residual passes
   530	    it, including one built from the wrong stat block, a doubled ML block, or a residual that
   531	    simply is not stat+ML at all. Naming a weak check after a strong claim is the same
   532	    "strong name over weak check" pattern as the test that passed because argparse rejected an
   533	    argument before the guard it named ever ran (verifier defect 6b). This compares."""
   534	    resid = np.asarray(Ccomb, float) - np.asarray(Csyst, float)
   535	    expect = np.asarray(Cstat, float) + np.asarray(Cml, float)
   536	    require(resid.shape == expect.shape,
   537	            f"full-total identity: residual {resid.shape} != stat+ML {expect.shape}")
   538	    # PSD of the residual is retained as a SEPARATE, weaker sanity check -- not as the identity.
   539	    check_symmetric_psd(resid)
   540	    return prove_identity(resid, expect, rtol, "C_combined - C_syst == C_stat + C_ML")

   543	def check_declared_migration_policy(policy, census_abs, band, nonzero_bands, zero_bands):
   544	    """Compare the DECLARED migration policy against the OBSERVED census.
   545	
   546	    REPAIR-5 (pattern sweep). `check_merged_metadata` required `migration_policy` to be
   547	    truthy and compared it to nothing -- a declared policy no consumer ever checked, which is
   548	    the same presence-not-comparison pattern as defect 2's `code_rev`. A merged file could
   549	    declare any policy string, or the wrong one, and pass."""
   550	    require(isinstance(policy, str) and policy.strip(), "declared migration policy missing")
   551	    require(band in nonzero_bands or band in zero_bands,
   552	            f"band {band} is in neither the nonzero- nor the zero-migration set")
   553	    n = int(census_abs)
   554	    if band in nonzero_bands:
   555	        require(n > 0, f"{band} declares selection-complete migration but census is {n}")
   556	        require("selection" in policy.lower(),
   557	                f"{band} migrates ({n}) but declares policy {policy!r}, which does not claim "
   558	                f"selection completeness")
   559	    else:
   560	        require(n == 0, f"{band} is declared bin-migration-only but census is {n}")
   561	        # REPAIR-6: the zero side never validated the policy TEXT, so a band could migrate zero
   562	        # events while declaring a selection-complete policy and pass.
   563	        require("selection" not in policy.lower() or "only" in policy.lower(),
   564	                f"{band} has zero selection migration but declares policy {policy!r}, which "
   565	                f"claims selection completeness")
   566	    return True

   569	def require_exact_endpoint_tags(tags):
   570	    """Reject BOTH missing and EXTRA tags. The old inventory only looked for the ten expected
   571	    names, so an eleventh product in the directory was invisible to it."""
   572	    got, want = set(tags), {f"{b}_{e}" for b in BANDS for e in ENDPOINTS}
   573	    require(not (want - got), f"missing endpoint tags: {sorted(want - got)}")
   574	    require(not (got - want), f"unexpected extra endpoint tags: {sorted(got - want)}")
   575	    return True

   578	def sha256_file(path, _bufsz=1 << 20):
   579	    """Durable file digest (login-computable; no ROOT)."""
   580	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   581	    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
   582	        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(_bufsz), b""):
   583	            h.update(chunk)
   584	    return h.hexdigest()

   587	def canonical_endpoints():
   588	    """The exact 10 (band, endpoint) logical tasks, in fixed order."""
   589	    return [(b, ep) for b in BANDS for ep in ENDPOINTS]
   590	
   591	
   592	def endpoint_manifest_hash(entries):
   593	    """entries: iterable of (band, endpoint, fingerprint). Hash is order-independent
   594	    over the SET but requires the exact 10-endpoint inventory (fail-closed)."""
   595	    seen = {}
   596	    for band, ep, fp in entries:
   597	        require(band in BANDS, f"unknown band {band!r}")
   598	        require(ep in ENDPOINTS, f"bad endpoint {ep!r}")
   599	        seen[(band, int(ep))] = str(fp)
   600	    require(len(seen) == N_ENDPOINTS,
   601	            f"expected exactly {N_ENDPOINTS} endpoints, got {len(seen)} "
   602	            f"(missing {sorted(set(canonical_endpoints())-set(seen))})")
   603	    blob = json.dumps({f"{b}_{e}": seen[(b, e)] for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()},
   604	                      sort_keys=True)
   605	    return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()

   607	
   608	def mask_order_hash(mask):
   609	    """Hash of the reported-bin mask + C-order. mask: 1-D bool/int array over the
   610	    full grid. Pins which bins are reported and their order so a projection or a
   611	    component sum can never silently mix masks."""
   612	    m = np.asarray(mask)
   613	    require(m.ndim == 1, "mask must be 1-D (C-order ravel over the 5D grid)")
   614	    require(m.size == GRID_NBINS, f"mask size {m.size} != grid {GRID_NBINS}")
   615	    idx = np.nonzero(m.astype(bool))[0].astype(np.int64)
   616	    require(idx.size > 0, "mask selects zero reported bins")
   617	    return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest(), int(idx.size)

   620	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- inventory gates
   621	def require_complete_unfold_set(present_tags):
   622	    """present_tags: iterable of '<band>_<ep>' that passed CONTENT validation.
   623	    Fail-closed unless all 10 canonical endpoints are present+valid."""
   624	    present = set(present_tags)
   625	    need = {f"{b}_{e}" for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()}
   626	    missing = sorted(need - present)
   627	    require(not missing, f"incomplete unfold set: missing/invalid {missing}")
   628	    require(present == need, f"unexpected unfold tags: {sorted(present - need)}")
   629	    return True

   632	def check_merged_metadata(meta):
   633	    """Merged-endpoint audit on extracted metadata (ROOT-free, so testable).
   634	    meta keys: tree_entries{mc_truth_denom,mc_signal_reco,mc_background,data},
   635	    mcPOT, dataPOT, hasTruthOnlyMisses, nTruthOnlyMisses,
   636	    census{TruthEntrants,TruthExits,RecoEntrants,RecoExits}, migration_policy."""
   637	    te = meta.get("tree_entries", {})
   638	    for t in ("mc_truth_denom", "mc_signal_reco", "mc_background", "data"):
   639	        require(te.get(t, 0) and te[t] > 0, f"merged tree {t} empty/absent")
   640	    mc, da = meta.get("mcPOT"), meta.get("dataPOT")
   641	    require(mc is not None and np.isfinite(mc) and mc > 0, "merged mcPOT not finite-positive")
   642	    require(da is not None and np.isfinite(da) and da > 0, "merged dataPOT not finite-positive")
   643	    require(te["mc_signal_reco"] == te["mc_truth_denom"],
   644	            f"signal_reco {te['mc_signal_reco']} != truth_denom {te['mc_truth_denom']} (completeness broken)")
   645	    # REPAIR-6: presence-only until now -- zero or mutually inconsistent values passed, though
   646	    # every real merged file carries a positive count. Repair-5 waived this on the grounds that
   647	    # no independent expected value existed without a physics read; the verifier was right that

   681	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- covariance gates
   682	def check_symmetric_psd(C, rtol_sym=1e-9, psd_atol_ratio=1e-12):
   683	    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=float)
   684	    require(C.ndim == 2 and C.shape[0] == C.shape[1], "covariance must be square 2-D")
   685	    require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), "covariance has non-finite entries")
   686	    denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(C)))
   687	    asym = np.max(np.abs(C - C.T)) / denom
   688	    require(asym <= rtol_sym, f"covariance not symmetric (rel {asym:.2e})")
   689	    ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(0.5 * (C + C.T))
   690	    require(ev[0] >= -psd_atol_ratio * abs(ev[-1]),
   691	            f"covariance not PSD (min/max eig {ev[0]/max(1e-300,abs(ev[-1])):.2e})")
   692	    d = np.diag(C)
   693	    # REPAIR-6, folding in BEN-044 from the PET lane ("an absolute tolerance inherited into a
   694	    # problem whose natural scale is ~1e-80 makes a gate that cannot fail"). This read
   695	    # `d >= -1e-30` ABSOLUTELY. The standard 5D covariance has sqrt(tr) ~ 5.3e-38 over 10694
   696	    # reported bins, so a typical diagonal entry is ~1e-79 -- the old bound sat ~49 orders of
   697	    # magnitude above what it was supposed to bound, and a diagonal of -1e-31 (a corruption
   698	    # ~48 orders larger than the signal) passed it. Now relative to the matrix's own scale.
   699	    # Power-proved in tests/test_p4_guard_mutations.py at the real 1e-79 scale, per BEN-044's
   700	    # rule that a gate must be shown able to fail in the commit that writes it.
   701	    require(np.all(np.isfinite(d)), "non-finite diagonal")
   702	    require(np.all(d >= -psd_atol_ratio * denom),
   703	            f"negative diagonal beyond tolerance (min {np.min(d):.3e} vs "
   704	            f"-{psd_atol_ratio:.0e} * max|C| = {-psd_atol_ratio * denom:.3e})")
   705	    return {"rel_asymmetry": float(asym), "min_eig": float(ev[0]), "max_eig": float(ev[-1]),
   706	            "diag_tol_absolute": float(psd_atol_ratio * denom)}
   707	
   708	
   709	def require_exact_bands(band_covs):
   710	    """band_covs: dict band -> cov. Must be EXACTLY the 5 kinematic bands."""
   711	    got = set(band_covs)
   712	    require(got == set(BANDS),
   713	            f"active-lateral bands mismatch: got {sorted(got)}, need {sorted(BANDS)}")
   714	    return True
   715	
   716	
   717	def component_traces_positive_finite(band_covs):
   718	    tr = {}
   719	    for b in BANDS:
   720	        require(b in band_covs, f"missing component band {b}")
   721	        C = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
   722	        require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), f"component {b} has non-finite entries")
   723	        t = float(np.trace(C))
   724	        require(np.isfinite(t) and t > 0.0, f"component {b} trace not positive-finite ({t})")
   725	        tr[b] = t
   726	    return tr
   727	
   728	
   729	def check_component_sum(total, band_covs, rtol=1e-9):
   730	    """total must equal the EXACT sum of the 5 per-band component covariances."""
   731	    require_exact_bands(band_covs)
   732	    total = np.asarray(total, dtype=float)
   733	    s = np.zeros_like(total)
   734	    for b in BANDS:
   735	        Cb = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
   736	        require(Cb.shape == total.shape, f"component {b} shape {Cb.shape} != total {total.shape}")
   737	        s = s + Cb
   738	    denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(total)))
   739	    err = np.max(np.abs(total - s)) / denom
   740	    require(err <= rtol, f"component sum mismatch (rel {err:.2e}) — total != sum of bands")
   741	    return float(err)
   742	
   743	
   744	def check_support_comparison(active_cov, support_cov):
   745	    """Complete support comparison: both present, same shape/mask/order. Returns
   746	    sqrt-trace ratio (active/support). Fail-closed if the support block is absent."""
   747	    require(active_cov is not None, "active lateral block missing")
   748	    require(support_cov is not None, "support-limited comparison block missing")
   749	    A = np.asarray(active_cov, dtype=float); S = np.asarray(support_cov, dtype=float)
   750	    require(A.shape == S.shape, f"active/support shape mismatch {A.shape} vs {S.shape}")
   751	    sta = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(A)))); sts = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(S))))
   752	    require(sts > 0, "support block has zero trace")
   753	    return {"sqrt_tr_active": sta, "sqrt_tr_support": sts, "ratio": sta / sts}
   754	
   755	
   756	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- deterministic projection map
   757	def build_projection_M(edges, drop_axis, mask_high, mask_low):
   758	    """Deterministic 5D->4D map by WIDTH-WEIGHTED marginalization of one axis.
   759	    edges: ordered per-axis edge arrays (pt,pz,eavail,q3,W). drop_axis: axis index to
   760	    marginalize (W=4). mask_high/mask_low: reported-bin bool masks over the full high/low
   761	    grids (C-order). Returns dense M (n_low_reported x n_high_reported) with
   762	    M[a,b] = width_drop[k] when high bin b decomposes to low bin a + dropped index k.
   763	    density convention: x_low = M @ x_high, C_low = M C_high M^T."""
   764	    nb = [np.asarray(e).size - 1 for e in edges]
   765	    require(len(nb) == 5, "expected 5 axes")
   766	    total_high = int(np.prod(nb))
   767	    require(int(np.asarray(mask_high).size) == total_high, "mask_high size != high grid")
   768	    wdrop = np.asarray(edges[drop_axis], float)[1:] - np.asarray(edges[drop_axis], float)[:-1]
   769	    nb_low = [n for i, n in enumerate(nb) if i != drop_axis]
   770	    total_low = int(np.prod(nb_low))
   771	    require(int(np.asarray(mask_low).size) == total_low, "mask_low size != low grid")
   772	    strides_h = np.array([int(np.prod(nb[i + 1:])) for i in range(5)])          # C-order strides
   773	    strides_l = np.array([int(np.prod(nb_low[i + 1:])) for i in range(4)])
   774	    mh = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask_high).astype(bool))[0]
   775	    ml = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask_low).astype(bool))[0]
   776	    low_pos = {int(g): r for r, g in enumerate(ml)}                              # low global -> reported row
   777	    M = np.zeros((ml.size, mh.size), dtype=float)
   778	    for col, g in enumerate(mh):
   779	        midx = [(g // strides_h[i]) % nb[i] for i in range(5)]                   # 5D multi-index
   780	        k = midx[drop_axis]
   781	        low_multi = [midx[i] for i in range(5) if i != drop_axis]
   782	        glow = int(np.dot(low_multi, strides_l))
   783	        row = low_pos.get(glow)
   784	        require(row is not None, f"high reported bin {g} maps to non-reported low bin {glow}")
   785	        M[row, col] = wdrop[k]
   786	    return M
   787	
   788	
   789	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- projection gates
   790	def project(C_high, M):
   791	    """5D->4D (or any) projection C_low = M C_high M^T (preserves density/order)."""
   792	    C_high = np.asarray(C_high, dtype=float); M = np.asarray(M, dtype=float)
   793	    require(M.shape[1] == C_high.shape[0], f"M cols {M.shape[1]} != C dim {C_high.shape[0]}")
   794	    return M @ C_high @ M.T
   795	
   796	
   797	def check_projection_nonmutation(C_high, M, x_high, x_low_frozen, rtol_central=3e-2):
   798	    """Enforce projected covariance validity AND central non-mutation:
   799	    C_low = M C_high M^T is symmetric/PSD, and M @ x_high reproduces the frozen
   800	    lower-dim central within tolerance (the projection must not mutate the central)."""
   801	    C_low = project(C_high, M)
   802	    stats = check_symmetric_psd(C_low)
   803	    xin = np.asarray(x_high, dtype=float); xfr = np.asarray(x_low_frozen, dtype=float)
   804	    proj = M @ xin
   805	    require(proj.shape == xfr.shape, f"projected central shape {proj.shape} != frozen {xfr.shape}")
   806	    denom = np.where(np.abs(xfr) > 0, np.abs(xfr), 1.0)
   807	    rel = float(np.max(np.abs(proj - xfr) / denom))
   808	    require(rel <= rtol_central, f"projection mutates central (max rel {rel:.2e})")
   809	    stats["central_max_rel"] = rel
   810	    return C_low, stats

   304	def prove_identity(A, B, rtol, label):
   305	    """Fail-closed max-relative-difference identity gate (no subtraction hidden)."""
   306	    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float); B = np.asarray(B, dtype=float)
   307	    require(A.shape == B.shape, f"{label}: shape {A.shape} != {B.shape}")
   308	    denom = max(1e-300, float(np.max(np.abs(A))))
   309	    err = float(np.max(np.abs(A - B)) / denom)
   310	    require(err <= rtol, f"{label}: identity broken (rel {err:.2e} > {rtol:.0e})")
   311	    return err

   314	def edges_bin_volume_hash(edges):
   315	    """Hash the ordered 5-axis edge arrays + the C-order per-bin volume vector."""
   316	    import numpy as _np
   317	    parts = []
   318	    for e in edges:
   319	        a = _np.asarray(e, dtype=float); require(a.ndim == 1 and a.size >= 2, "bad edge array")
   320	        parts.append(a.tobytes())
   321	    edge_hash = hashlib.sha256(b"|".join(parts)).hexdigest()
   322	    widths = [(_np.asarray(e, float)[1:] - _np.asarray(e, float)[:-1]) for e in edges]
   323	    vol = widths[0]
   324	    for w in widths[1:]:
   325	        vol = _np.multiply.outer(vol, w).ravel()   # C-order product of bin widths
   326	    vol_hash = hashlib.sha256(vol.astype(float).tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()
   327	    return {"edge_hash": edge_hash, "bin_volume_hash": vol_hash, "n_bins": int(vol.size)}

   330	def validate_orchestrator_merged_receipt(recdir, live_stat):
   331	    """Consume the owner-neutral merged-hash receipt: require COMPLETE + the four
   332	    files, recompute live size/integer-mtime vs the inventory, and return the
   333	    committed hash-list digest bound into our manifest. live_stat: dict path->(size,mtime)."""
   334	    import os as _os
   335	    for f in ("COMPLETE", "summary.tsv", "validation.tsv", "standard.sha256", "standard.inventory.tsv"):
   336	        require(_os.path.exists(_os.path.join(recdir, f)), f"orchestrator receipt missing {f}")
   337	    sha_lines = [l for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.sha256")).read().splitlines() if l.strip()]
   338	    require(len(sha_lines) == N_ENDPOINTS, f"receipt standard.sha256 has {len(sha_lines)} != 10 lines")
   339	    merged = {}
   340	    for l in sha_lines:
   341	        h, p = l.split(None, 1); merged[p.strip()] = h
   342	    # recompute live size + integer mtime against the committed inventory
   343	    inv = {}
   344	    for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.inventory.tsv")).read().splitlines():
   345	        if not l.strip() or l.startswith("#"):
   346	            continue
   347	        cols = l.split("\t")
   348	        if len(cols) >= 3:                       # orchestrator format: size<TAB>mtime<TAB>path
   349	            inv[cols[2]] = (int(cols[0]), int(float(cols[1])))
   350	    for p, (sz, mt) in inv.items():
   351	        require(p in live_stat, f"inventory path not live: {p}")
   352	        lsz, lmt = live_stat[p]
   353	        require(lsz == sz and int(lmt) == mt, f"live size/mtime drift for {p}")
   354	    digest = hashlib.sha256("\n".join(sorted(f"{merged[p]}  {p}" for p in merged)).encode()).hexdigest()
   355	    return {"merged_sha256": merged, "hash_list_digest": digest, "n": len(merged)}

   358	# ------------------------------------------- endpoint receipt schema (repair-4, defect 2)
   359	# Before repair-4 the resume path skipped an endpoint on `[[ -s ROOT && -s RECEIPT ]]` plus a
   360	# ROOT-key/dimension check. Receipt tag, ROOT sha, merged sha, central sha, config hash and
   361	# source provenance were never read, and the legacy-attest receipt did not even record the
   362	# merged/central hashes -- so "resumable" meant "any nonempty file pair is accepted forever".
   363	# That is the same size-as-completion family as BEN-023 and KNOWN_ISSUES #20(c).
   364	#
   365	# NOTE ON COST: the merged inputs are 53.8 GB each (538 GB total). This validator therefore
   366	# does NOT re-hash them; it compares the receipt's recorded merged sha against the
   367	# owner-neutral orchestrator receipt's committed hash, whose own live size+integer-mtime check
   368	# is what detects a changed input. Same reuse p4_evidence.py already relies on. The ROOT and
   369	# central files are ~480 KB and ARE re-hashed live on every skip.
   370	RECEIPT_MODES = ("produced", "legacy-attested")
   371	RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS = ("tag", "mode", "root_sha256", "merged_sha256", "central5d_sha256",
   372	                         "config_hash", "bkg_mode", "code_rev", "unfold_blob", "t")
   373	# The source whose blob decides whether a cached endpoint is still valid. The unfold driver is
   374	# the one that actually produces the ROOT; the others in p4_evidence.SRC describe downstream
   375	# consumers and do not invalidate an endpoint.
   376	RECEIPT_SOURCE_KEY = "unfold_blob"

   404	def paths_unchanged_between(rev_a, rev_b, paths):
   405	    """Every path in `paths` has the same blob at rev_a and rev_b.
   406	
   407	    Returns (ok, [differing paths]). Fails CLOSED: if git cannot answer for a path, that path
   408	    counts as differing, because an unverifiable claim is not a satisfied one."""
   409	    differing = []
   410	    for p in paths:
   411	        try:
   412	            a = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{rev_a}:{p}"], cwd=REPO_ROOT,
   413	                                        text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   414	        except Exception:
   415	            a = None
   416	        try:
   417	            b = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{rev_b}:{p}"], cwd=REPO_ROOT,
   418	                                        text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   419	        except Exception:
   420	            b = None
   421	        if a is None or b is None or a != b:
   422	            differing.append(p)
   423	    return (not differing), differing

   426	def code_rev_in_history(rev, head=None):
   427	    """True iff `rev` is reachable from HEAD in this repository (ancestor of, or equal to).
   428	
   429	    Deliberately reachability rather than equality -- see the note in validate_endpoint_receipt.
   430	    Fails CLOSED: if git cannot answer, the answer is False, because an unverifiable provenance
   431	    claim is not a satisfied one."""
   432	    if not isinstance(rev, str) or not rev.strip():
   433	        return False
   434	    try:
   435	        subprocess.check_call(["git", "merge-base", "--is-ancestor", rev.strip(), head or "HEAD"],
   436	                              cwd=REPO_ROOT, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   437	        return True
   438	    except Exception:
   439	        return False

   441	
   442	def validate_endpoint_receipt(rec, *, tag, root_sha256, merged_sha256,
   443	                              central5d_sha256, config_hash, bkg_mode,
   444	                              code_rev, unfold_blob):
   445	    """Fail closed unless the receipt is complete AND every recorded identity matches the
   446	    live/committed one. Returns True, or raises P4GateError naming the first mismatch.
   447	
   448	    REPAIR-5 (defect 2). The previous version required `code_rev` to be a NON-EMPTY STRING and
   449	    compared it to nothing, and the receipt recorded no source identity at all -- so an
   450	    endpoint produced under a changed unfold driver was still skipped. That is the third
   451	    instance in this chain of "assert presence, never compare": the first is the
   452	    `P4_VERIFIER_PASS` non-emptiness gate (KNOWN_ISSUES #21), the second is the `identities`
   453	    block whose members were literal `True`. Presence is not evidence; both `code_rev` and the
   454	    producing driver's committed blob are now COMPARED."""
   455	    require(isinstance(rec, dict) and rec, f"receipt for {tag} is not a JSON object")
   456	    missing = [k for k in RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS if k not in rec]
   457	    require(not missing, f"receipt {tag} missing required keys {missing} (incomplete legacy format)")
   458	    require(rec["mode"] in RECEIPT_MODES, f"receipt {tag} unknown mode {rec['mode']!r}")
   459	    require(rec["tag"] == tag, f"receipt tag {rec['tag']!r} != {tag!r} (receipt/ROOT pair mismatch)")
   460	    require(rec["root_sha256"] == root_sha256,
   461	            f"receipt {tag} root_sha256 drift: recorded {rec['root_sha256']} vs live {root_sha256}")
   462	    require(rec["merged_sha256"] == merged_sha256,
   463	            f"receipt {tag} merged_sha256 drift vs the orchestrator receipt")
   464	    require(rec["central5d_sha256"] == central5d_sha256,
   465	            f"receipt {tag} central5d_sha256 drift (the central moved under this endpoint)")
   466	    require(rec["config_hash"] == config_hash,
   467	            f"receipt {tag} config_hash drift: produced under a different unfold configuration")
   468	    require(rec["bkg_mode"] == bkg_mode,
   469	            f"receipt {tag} bkg_mode {rec['bkg_mode']!r} != declared {bkg_mode!r}")
   470	    # D2: compared, not merely present.
   471	    require(isinstance(rec["code_rev"], str) and rec["code_rev"],
   472	            f"receipt {tag} has no code_rev")
   473	    require(isinstance(code_rev, str) and code_rev,
   474	            f"cannot validate receipt {tag}: no live code_rev to compare against")
   475	    # REPAIR-6b. This used to require code_rev == HEAD, and that was WRONG in the same way
   476	    # KNOWN_ISSUES #24 describes: it broke on correct behaviour. Any commit anywhere in the repo
   477	    # -- including another lane's, touching nothing this chain reads -- expired all ten receipts
   478	    # while the code that produced them was byte-identical. Caught by the self-check on the first
   479	    # production run: the PET lane advanced main by 8 commits mid-run, HEAD moved
   480	    # 42268b6 -> 203ff01, `git diff` on the unfold driver was EMPTY, and the gate rejected all ten.
   481	    #
   482	    # The producing-code binding is `unfold_blob`, which IS strictly compared below. `code_rev` is
   483	    # repository context, so the honest check is REACHABILITY, not equality: the receipt must come
   484	    # from this history. That still catches a receipt carried in from a foreign branch or a
   485	    # rewritten history, and does not expire on unrelated work.
   486	    require(code_rev_in_history(rec["code_rev"]),
   487	            f"receipt {tag} code_rev {rec['code_rev']} is not an ancestor of HEAD ({code_rev}): "
   488	            f"this receipt did not come from this repository's history")

   196	# ---- source provenance: git blobs of code + binary digest + per-file source commits ----
   197	import subprocess
   198	# REPAIR-4 (verifier defect 3b/3c, and KNOWN_ISSUES #23). These used to hash the WORKING TREE
   199	# (`git hash-object <path>`), so the manifest recorded whatever happened to be checked out --
   200	# which is how an unrelated dirty OmniFold blob was absorbed in 2026-07, and how re-running
   201	# evidence in 2026-08 silently re-attributed 07-18 endpoints to a newer driver and binary.
   202	# Now: hash the COMMITTED object at HEAD, record the commit that introduced that exact blob,
   203	# and fail closed if the working tree differs from HEAD for any bound path -- because a
   204	# provenance record that can disagree with what actually ran is worse than none.
   205	def _committed_blob(rel):
   206	    """The blob sha of `rel` as committed at HEAD (not the working-tree copy)."""
   207	    try:
   208	        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"HEAD:{rel}"],
   209	                                       cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   210	    except Exception:
   211	        return None
   212	def _worktree_blob(rel):
   213	    try:
   214	        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "hash-object", rel],
   215	                                       cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   216	    except Exception:
   217	        return None
   218	def _blob_introducing_commit(rel, blob):
   219	    """The most recent commit whose version of `rel` IS `blob`. The old code recorded
   220	    `git log -1 -- <rel>`, i.e. the last commit to touch the path, which need not be the commit
   221	    that introduced the blob it recorded beside it."""
   222	    if not blob:
   223	        return None
   224	    try:
   225	        revs = subprocess.check_output(["git", "log", "--format=%H", "--", rel],
   226	                                       cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).split()
   227	        for r in revs:
   228	            try:
   229	                b = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{r}:{rel}"],
   230	                                            cwd=REPO, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
   231	            except Exception:
   232	                continue
   233	            if b == blob:
   234	                return r
   235	    except Exception:
   236	        pass
   237	    return None

   246	                         for k, v in SRC.items()}

   267	        # `_c == None` -> False -> blocker, which is the correct direction.
   268	        need(_c == _w,
   269	             f"source {_k} ({_rel}) is DIRTY: working tree {_w} != committed {_c}. Commit or revert "
   270	             f"before generating evidence -- a manifest that records the committed blob while a "
   271	             f"different file runs is the 2026-07 dirty-OmniFold absorption (defect 3b).")
