 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	# STANDARD P4 stage-1: 10 endpoint hadd-merges (CONC-capped for Lustre) + acceptance
     3	# audit. Runs inside a held interactive CPU alloc (via alloc_run srun --overlap; the
     4	# whole node is ours, so bare-background hadds are fine). skip-if-exists.
     5	set -o pipefail
     6	export HOME=/global/homes/j/josephrb
     7	REPO="/pscratch/sd/j/josephrb/MINERvA-OmniFold"; ND="${REPO}/nd-unfolding"
     8	PLAYLISTS=(1A 1B 1C 1D 1E 1F 1G 1L 1M 1N 1O 1P)
     9	BANDS=(BeamAngleX BeamAngleY MuonResolution Muon_Energy_MINERvA Muon_Energy_MINOS)
    10	MERGEDIR="${ND}/active_universe_5d/standard/merged"; mkdir -p "${MERGEDIR}"
    11	CONC="${CONC:-4}"
    12	echo "[p4-merge] start $(date -u +%T) CONC=${CONC}"
    13	valid_merged () {  # a complete merged ROOT opens with the 4 trees
    14	  python3 -c "import ROOT,sys; f=ROOT.TFile.Open('$1'); sys.exit(0 if (f and not f.IsZombie() and f.Get('mc_truth_denom') and f.Get('mc_signal_reco') and f.Get('mc_background') and f.Get('data')) else 1)" >/dev/null 2>&1
    15	}
    16	merge_one () {
    17	  local BAND="$1" EP="$2"
    18	  local MERGED="${MERGEDIR}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_${BAND}_${EP}.root"
    19	  if [[ -s "${MERGED}" ]] && valid_merged "${MERGED}"; then echo "[merge] SKIP ${BAND}:${EP} valid ($(stat -c '%s' "${MERGED}")B)"; return 0; fi
    20	  [[ -s "${MERGED}" ]] && echo "[merge] REDO ${BAND}:${EP} (existing invalid/partial)" && rm -f "${MERGED}"
    21	  local INPUTS=(); for PL in "${PLAYLISTS[@]}"; do local f="${ND}/active_universe_5d/standard/${BAND}_${EP}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_${PL}_active_${BAND}_${EP}.root"; [[ -s "$f" ]] && INPUTS+=("$f"); done
    22	  [[ ${#INPUTS[@]} -ne 12 ]] && { echo "[merge] ABORT ${BAND}:${EP} ${#INPUTS[@]}/12"; return 3; }
    23	  python "${REPO}/2d-unfolding/uq/hadd_universes_full.py" "${MERGED}" "${INPUTS[@]}" > "${MERGEDIR}/merge_${BAND}_${EP}.log" 2>&1 \
    24	    && echo "[merge] DONE ${BAND}:${EP} ($(stat -c '%s' "${MERGED}")B)" || { echo "[merge] FAIL ${BAND}:${EP}"; rm -f "${MERGED}"; return 4; }
    25	}
    26	# J31 (AUDIT-FINDINGS-20260731): merge failures used to be converted into success, twice over.
    27	# `merge_one`'s last command on the failure path was `rm -f`, which returns 0 whether or not the
    28	# file exists, so the function exited 0; and a bare `wait` discards every child status anyway,
    29	# including the `return 3` abort the author did write. NMERGED was computed, printed, and never
    30	# compared to EXPECTED, and there is no `set -e`. Fixing only the `rm` would have changed nothing.
    31	# So: collect the PIDs, wait on each one individually, and fail closed on the count.
    32	EXPECTED=$(( ${#BANDS[@]} * 2 ))
    33	PIDS=(); LABELS=()
    34	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
    35	  while [ "$(jobs -rp | wc -l)" -ge "$CONC" ]; do sleep 5; done
    36	  merge_one "$BAND" "$EP" &
    37	  PIDS+=($!); LABELS+=("${BAND}:${EP}")
    38	done; done
    39	NFAILED=0
    40	for i in "${!PIDS[@]}"; do
    41	  if ! wait "${PIDS[$i]}"; then
    42	    echo "[merge] child ${LABELS[$i]} exited non-zero (rc=$?)" >&2
    43	    NFAILED=$(( NFAILED + 1 ))
    44	  fi
    45	done
    46	NMERGED=$(find "${MERGEDIR}" -name 'runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_*.root' -size +0c 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    47	echo "[p4-merge] merges done $(date -u +%T); merged=${NMERGED}/${EXPECTED}; failed_children=${NFAILED}"
    48	if [[ "${NFAILED}" -ne 0 || "${NMERGED}" -ne "${EXPECTED}" ]]; then
    49	  echo "[p4-merge][FAIL] ${NMERGED}/${EXPECTED} merged, ${NFAILED} child failure(s) -- refusing to" >&2
    50	  echo "  run the acceptance audit on an incomplete merge set. Re-run; merge_one is idempotent" >&2
    51	  echo "  (it SKIPs a valid merged file and REDOes an invalid one)." >&2
    52	  exit 5
    53	fi
    54	echo "[p4-audit] running acceptance audit..."
    55	cd "${ND}" && python3 p3s_manifest_summary.py --mode standard
    56	echo "[p4-merge-audit] complete $(date -u +%T)"

commit c31f5d63eb8572f23ed84fa5bf7db89f9a830e77
Author:     Joseph Bailey <jrbailey555@gmail.com>
AuthorDate: Fri Aug 7 19:19:41 2026 -0400
Commit:     Joseph Bailey <jrbailey555@gmail.com>
CommitDate: Fri Aug 7 19:19:41 2026 -0400

    Repair-6 fixes: enforce the cross-check, recompute C_syst, and stop three checks from opting out
    
    verifier_crosscheck is now ENFORCED. It was computed against the independently-observed
    bindings, printed as MATCH/DIFF, and never checked -- all five could read DIFF and the stage
    still exited EVIDENCE-COMPLETE. Found by the mechanical sweep, on no prior list.
    
    C_syst is now RECOMPUTED from the persisted retained components plus the active total, closing
    the verifier's finding 2. The builder recorded C_syst_eq_retained_plus_active_relerr and
    neither consumer read it, so a wrong-but-PSD C_syst satisfying C_combined = C_syst+C_stat+C_ML
    passed everything.
    
    Native-miss fields are compared, not merely present: a 0/1 flag, a positive count, and the two
    must agree. Repair-5 waived this claiming no independent expected value existed; that
    overstated it, and the real files already satisfy the comparison.
    
    complete_support_comparison is relabelled support_trace_ratio_recorded. The check compares
    traces and bounds nothing, so "complete" claimed more than it delivers -- and BEN-043 rule 3
    adds that an aggregate cannot see a per-bin disagreement preserving the trace.
    
    The migration-policy comparison is no longer conditional on optional fields, which let any
    caller opt out by omission, and the zero-migration side now validates the policy TEXT too.
    
    check_symmetric_psd's bare `d >= -1e-30` is now relative to max|C|, per BEN-044. Severity is
    recorded honestly as redundant rather than exploitable: for a symmetric matrix
    min(diag) >= min(eigenvalue), so the relative PSD check above it already rejected the same
    corruption, and the tests demonstrate that rather than asserting a hole I did not find.
    
    Found while doing the above, and recorded as a THIRD pattern rather than buried:
    p4_lib.check_merged_metadata has NO production caller -- only tests. p4_evidence.py
    re-implements the same checks inline. So both repair-5's and repair-6's improvements to it
    improved a function that does not run. Pattern C, "a gate that is never invoked": coverage
    looks green and it protects nothing. Collapsing the duplication changes p4_evidence's
    accumulate-vs-raise semantics, so it is marked FIX and deferred to its own commit rather than
    ridden in on this one.
    
    135 tests pass.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

 def cmask_order_hash_4d(mask):
     """4D reported-mask + C-order fingerprint (repair-4, defect 5c).
 
     `mask_order_hash` fails closed on GRID_NBINS, which is the 5D grid, so the 4D mask had no
     hash helper and the projector compared only the reported COUNT. A count is not an ordering:
     a permuted 4D mask with the same population passed. Same construction as the 5D helper --
     C-order indices of the selected bins -- so the two are comparable by eye in a manifest.
     Deliberately NOT size-pinned: the 4D grid is a marginal of the 5D one and the standard lane
     has no pinned canonical 4D target (see RUNBOOK-20260807 §7.4)."""
     idx = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask))[0].astype(np.int64)
     require(idx.size > 0, "4D reported mask is empty")
     return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()
 
 
 def matrix_content_hash(M):
     """Deterministic digest of a matrix's CONTENTS (repair-4, defect 5d).
 
     The projection receipt recorded only `M_shape`, which two different projectors of the same
     dimensions share. Hashes C-contiguous float64 bytes plus the shape, so a changed weight is
     caught and a transposed/reshaped M cannot collide with the original."""
     A = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(M, dtype=np.float64))
     h = hashlib.sha256()
     h.update(repr(A.shape).encode()); h.update(b"|C|f8|"); h.update(A.tobytes())
     return h.hexdigest()
 
 
 def check_full_total_identity(Ccomb, Csyst, Cstat, Cml, rtol=1e-9):
     """PROVE C_combined = C_syst + C_stat + C_ML by comparing the residual to the actual
     stat and ML blocks. Returns the measured max-relative error.
 
     REPAIR-5 (defect 4b / 6). Repair-4 checked only that `C_combined - C_syst` was symmetric
     PSD and called that the full-total identity, in a gate name, a receipt field and a test
     name. PSD is NECESSARY but nowhere near SUFFICIENT: every covariance-shaped residual passes
     it, including one built from the wrong stat block, a doubled ML block, or a residual that
     simply is not stat+ML at all. Naming a weak check after a strong claim is the same
     "strong name over weak check" pattern as the test that passed because argparse rejected an
     argument before the guard it named ever ran (verifier defect 6b). This compares."""
     resid = np.asarray(Ccomb, float) - np.asarray(Csyst, float)
     expect = np.asarray(Cstat, float) + np.asarray(Cml, float)
     require(resid.shape == expect.shape,
             f"full-total identity: residual {resid.shape} != stat+ML {expect.shape}")
     # PSD of the residual is retained as a SEPARATE, weaker sanity check -- not as the identity.
     check_symmetric_psd(resid)
     return prove_identity(resid, expect, rtol, "C_combined - C_syst == C_stat + C_ML")
 
 
 def check_declared_migration_policy(policy, census_abs, band, nonzero_bands, zero_bands):
     """Compare the DECLARED migration policy against the OBSERVED census.
 
     REPAIR-5 (pattern sweep). `check_merged_metadata` required `migration_policy` to be
     truthy and compared it to nothing -- a declared policy no consumer ever checked, which is
     the same presence-not-comparison pattern as defect 2's `code_rev`. A merged file could
     declare any policy string, or the wrong one, and pass."""
     require(isinstance(policy, str) and policy.strip(), "declared migration policy missing")
     require(band in nonzero_bands or band in zero_bands,
             f"band {band} is in neither the nonzero- nor the zero-migration set")
     n = int(census_abs)
     if band in nonzero_bands:
         require(n > 0, f"{band} declares selection-complete migration but census is {n}")
         require("selection" in policy.lower(),
                 f"{band} migrates ({n}) but declares policy {policy!r}, which does not claim "
                 f"selection completeness")
     else:
         require(n == 0, f"{band} is declared bin-migration-only but census is {n}")
+        # REPAIR-6: the zero side never validated the policy TEXT, so a band could migrate zero
+        # events while declaring a selection-complete policy and pass.
+        require("selection" not in policy.lower() or "only" in policy.lower(),
+                f"{band} has zero selection migration but declares policy {policy!r}, which "
+                f"claims selection completeness")
     return True
 
 
 def require_exact_endpoint_tags(tags):
     """Reject BOTH missing and EXTRA tags. The old inventory only looked for the ten expected
     names, so an eleventh product in the directory was invisible to it."""
     got, want = set(tags), {f"{b}_{e}" for b in BANDS for e in ENDPOINTS}
     require(not (want - got), f"missing endpoint tags: {sorted(want - got)}")
     require(not (got - want), f"unexpected extra endpoint tags: {sorted(got - want)}")
     return True
 
 
 def sha256_file(path, _bufsz=1 << 20):
     """Durable file digest (login-computable; no ROOT)."""
     h = hashlib.sha256()
     with open(path, "rb") as fh:
         for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(_bufsz), b""):
             h.update(chunk)
     return h.hexdigest()
 
 
 def canonical_endpoints():
     """The exact 10 (band, endpoint) logical tasks, in fixed order."""
     return [(b, ep) for b in BANDS for ep in ENDPOINTS]
 
 
 def endpoint_manifest_hash(entries):
     """entries: iterable of (band, endpoint, fingerprint). Hash is order-independent
     over the SET but requires the exact 10-endpoint inventory (fail-closed)."""
     seen = {}
     for band, ep, fp in entries:
         require(band in BANDS, f"unknown band {band!r}")
         require(ep in ENDPOINTS, f"bad endpoint {ep!r}")
         seen[(band, int(ep))] = str(fp)
     require(len(seen) == N_ENDPOINTS,
             f"expected exactly {N_ENDPOINTS} endpoints, got {len(seen)} "
             f"(missing {sorted(set(canonical_endpoints())-set(seen))})")
     blob = json.dumps({f"{b}_{e}": seen[(b, e)] for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()},
                       sort_keys=True)
     return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()
 
 
 def mask_order_hash(mask):
     """Hash of the reported-bin mask + C-order. mask: 1-D bool/int array over the
     full grid. Pins which bins are reported and their order so a projection or a
     component sum can never silently mix masks."""
     m = np.asarray(mask)
     require(m.ndim == 1, "mask must be 1-D (C-order ravel over the 5D grid)")
     require(m.size == GRID_NBINS, f"mask size {m.size} != grid {GRID_NBINS}")
     idx = np.nonzero(m.astype(bool))[0].astype(np.int64)
     require(idx.size > 0, "mask selects zero reported bins")
     return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest(), int(idx.size)
 
 
 # ---------------------------------------------------------------- inventory gates
 def require_complete_unfold_set(present_tags):
     """present_tags: iterable of '<band>_<ep>' that passed CONTENT validation.
     Fail-closed unless all 10 canonical endpoints are present+valid."""
     present = set(present_tags)
     need = {f"{b}_{e}" for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()}
     missing = sorted(need - present)
     require(not missing, f"incomplete unfold set: missing/invalid {missing}")
     require(present == need, f"unexpected unfold tags: {sorted(present - need)}")
     return True

 # ---------------------------------------------------------------- covariance gates
 def check_symmetric_psd(C, rtol_sym=1e-9, psd_atol_ratio=1e-12):
     C = np.asarray(C, dtype=float)
     require(C.ndim == 2 and C.shape[0] == C.shape[1], "covariance must be square 2-D")
     require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), "covariance has non-finite entries")
     denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(C)))
     asym = np.max(np.abs(C - C.T)) / denom
     require(asym <= rtol_sym, f"covariance not symmetric (rel {asym:.2e})")
     ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(0.5 * (C + C.T))
     require(ev[0] >= -psd_atol_ratio * abs(ev[-1]),
             f"covariance not PSD (min/max eig {ev[0]/max(1e-300,abs(ev[-1])):.2e})")
     d = np.diag(C)
     # REPAIR-6, folding in BEN-044 from the PET lane ("an absolute tolerance inherited into a
     # problem whose natural scale is ~1e-80 makes a gate that cannot fail"). This read
     # `d >= -1e-30` ABSOLUTELY. The standard 5D covariance has sqrt(tr) ~ 5.3e-38 over 10694
     # reported bins, so a typical diagonal entry is ~1e-79 -- the old bound sat ~49 orders of
     # magnitude above what it was supposed to bound, and a diagonal of -1e-31 (a corruption
     # ~48 orders larger than the signal) passed it. Now relative to the matrix's own scale.
     # Power-proved in tests/test_p4_guard_mutations.py at the real 1e-79 scale, per BEN-044's
     # rule that a gate must be shown able to fail in the commit that writes it.
     require(np.all(np.isfinite(d)), "non-finite diagonal")
     require(np.all(d >= -psd_atol_ratio * denom),
             f"negative diagonal beyond tolerance (min {np.min(d):.3e} vs "
             f"-{psd_atol_ratio:.0e} * max|C| = {-psd_atol_ratio * denom:.3e})")
     return {"rel_asymmetry": float(asym), "min_eig": float(ev[0]), "max_eig": float(ev[-1]),
             "diag_tol_absolute": float(psd_atol_ratio * denom)}
 
 
 def require_exact_bands(band_covs):
     """band_covs: dict band -> cov. Must be EXACTLY the 5 kinematic bands."""
     got = set(band_covs)
     require(got == set(BANDS),
             f"active-lateral bands mismatch: got {sorted(got)}, need {sorted(BANDS)}")
     return True
 
 
 def component_traces_positive_finite(band_covs):
     tr = {}
     for b in BANDS:
         require(b in band_covs, f"missing component band {b}")
         C = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
         require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), f"component {b} has non-finite entries")
         t = float(np.trace(C))
         require(np.isfinite(t) and t > 0.0, f"component {b} trace not positive-finite ({t})")
         tr[b] = t
     return tr
 
 
 def check_component_sum(total, band_covs, rtol=1e-9):
     """total must equal the EXACT sum of the 5 per-band component covariances."""
     require_exact_bands(band_covs)
     total = np.asarray(total, dtype=float)
     s = np.zeros_like(total)
     for b in BANDS:
         Cb = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
         require(Cb.shape == total.shape, f"component {b} shape {Cb.shape} != total {total.shape}")
         s = s + Cb
     denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(total)))
     err = np.max(np.abs(total - s)) / denom
     require(err <= rtol, f"component sum mismatch (rel {err:.2e}) — total != sum of bands")
     return float(err)
 
 
 def check_support_comparison(active_cov, support_cov):
     """Complete support comparison: both present, same shape/mask/order. Returns
     sqrt-trace ratio (active/support). Fail-closed if the support block is absent."""
     require(active_cov is not None, "active lateral block missing")
     require(support_cov is not None, "support-limited comparison block missing")
     A = np.asarray(active_cov, dtype=float); S = np.asarray(support_cov, dtype=float)
     require(A.shape == S.shape, f"active/support shape mismatch {A.shape} vs {S.shape}")
     sta = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(A)))); sts = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(S))))
     require(sts > 0, "support block has zero trace")
     return {"sqrt_tr_active": sta, "sqrt_tr_support": sts, "ratio": sta / sts}

         out["gates"].append("symmetric_psd")
         # D4b/D6 REPAIR-5: repair-4 checked only that (C_combined - C_syst) was symmetric PSD

         def _bound_block(spec, sha_key, label):
             """Load a `path:key` block AFTER re-verifying the sha256 the manifest recorded, so
             the identity cannot be satisfied by substituting a different file."""
             P.require(":" in spec, f"{label} spec {spec!r} is not path:key")
             pth, key = spec.rsplit(":", 1)
             ap = pth if os.path.isabs(pth) else os.path.join(P.ND_ROOT, pth)
             P.require(os.path.exists(ap), f"{label} covariance not found: {pth}")
             P.require(P.sha256_file(ap) == comp.get(sha_key),
                       f"{label} covariance sha256 drift vs the component manifest ({pth})")
             blk = _th2(ap, key)
             P.require(blk is not None, f"{label} covariance key {key} missing in {pth}")
             return blk
 
         C_stat = _bound_block(stat_spec, "stat_sha256", "stat")
         C_ml = _bound_block(ml_spec, "ml_sha256", "ML")
         out["full_total_identity_relerr"] = float(
             P.check_full_total_identity(Ccomb, Csyst, C_stat, C_ml, 1e-9))
         out["gates"].append("full_total_identity_recomputed")
+
+        # REPAIR-6: C_syst itself was never recomputed. The builder recorded
+        # C_syst_eq_retained_plus_active_relerr and NEITHER consumer read it, so a wrong-but-PSD
+        # C_syst satisfying C_combined = C_syst + C_stat + C_ML passed every gate. It is
+        # recomputable because repair-4 (4a) started persisting the retained components into the
+        # candidate -- which made the omission worse, not better.
+        retained_keys = [k for k in comp.get("candidate_keys", [])
+                         if k.startswith("hCov_retained5d_")]
+        P.require(retained_keys,
+                  "candidate persists no retained components, so C_syst cannot be recomputed "
+                  "(pre-repair-4 candidate?)")
+        retained_sum = None
+        for k in retained_keys:
+            blk = _th2(a.candidate, k)
+            P.require(blk is not None, f"candidate is missing declared retained component {k}")
+            retained_sum = blk if retained_sum is None else retained_sum + blk
+        active_total_blk = _th2(a.candidate, P.CANDIDATE_ACTIVE_TOTAL_KEY)
+        out["c_syst_identity_relerr"] = float(
+            P.prove_identity(Csyst, retained_sum + active_total_blk, 1e-9,
+                             "C_syst == sum(retained) + active_total"))
+        out["n_retained_components"] = len(retained_keys)
+        out["gates"].append("c_syst_recomputed_from_components")
         support_bands = {b: _th2(a.support, f"hCov_universe5d_{b}") for b in P.BANDS}
         P.require(all(support_bands[b] is not None for b in P.BANDS), "support family lateral block incomplete")
         out["support_comparison"] = P.check_support_comparison(active_total, sum(support_bands[b] for b in P.BANDS))
-        out["gates"].append("complete_support_comparison")
+        # REPAIR-6: relabelled. The check compares TRACES and bounds nothing -- any finite ratio
+        # passes -- so calling it "complete" claimed more than it delivers, and BEN-043 rule 3
+        # adds that an aggregate cannot see a per-bin disagreement that preserves the trace.
+        # The ratio stays as a recorded DIAGNOSTIC; the gate name no longer asserts a comparison.
+        out["support_ratio_is_diagnostic_not_bounded"] = True
+        out["gates"].append("support_trace_ratio_recorded")
         out["result"] = "PASS"
     except P.P4GateError as e:
         out["error"] = str(e); json.dump(out, open(a.out, "w"), indent=2)
         print(f"RESULT FAIL :: {e}"); sys.exit(1)
     json.dump(out, open(a.out, "w"), indent=2)
     print("RESULT PASS — gates:", ",".join(out["gates"]),
           f"support_ratio={out['support_comparison']['ratio']:.3f}")
     sys.exit(0)
 
 
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     main()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""P4 standard CANONICAL component builder — manifest-bound, PURE-ADDITION, FAIL-CLOSED
     3	(repair round 3, 2026-07-18).
     4	
     5	No subtraction anywhere. Enumerates EVERY corrected background-aware per-band universe
     6	component by exact key + content hash, proves the corrected identities
     7	
     8	    C_syst      == sum(all corrected per-band components)              (from support family)
     9	    C_combined  == C_syst + C_stat + C_ML                              (stat/ML from SEPARATE ROOTs)
    10	
    11	then builds the candidate by dropping only the 5 named support LATERAL bands and ADDING
    12	the 5 selection-complete active per-band MAT blocks:
    13	
    14	    C_syst_active     = sum(retained non-lateral bands) + sum(5 active MAT bands)
    15	    C_combined_active = C_syst_active + C_stat + C_ML
    16	
    17	Persists in ROOT: 5 active per-band keys, active-only total, C_syst_active, C_combined_active;
    18	and in JSON: every retained pure component key + content hash + source, active traces, and
    19	all identities. Candidate path only. NOT run in the repair round (candidate construction is
    20	gated on the standard-p4-verifier PASS).
    21	"""
    22	import argparse, json, os, sys
    23	import numpy as np
    24	import p4_lib as P
    25	from uq_math import mat_covariance
    26	
    27	BKGAWARE_DIR = "uq_5d/universe_stage2_5d_bkgaware"
    28	SUPERSEDED_TOKENS = ("universe_stage2_5d/uq_universe_5d_covariance_combined.root", "_prehm_", "uthrow_slabs")
    29	SUPPORT_LATERAL = set(P.BANDS)           # the 5 bands replaced by active blocks

    31	
    32	def _th2(path, key):
    33	    import ROOT
    34	    f = ROOT.TFile.Open(path)
    35	    if not f or f.IsZombie():
    36	        raise P.P4GateError(f"cannot open {path}")
    37	    h = f.Get(key)
    38	    if not h:
    39	        f.Close(); return None
    40	    n = h.GetNbinsX()
    41	    arr = np.frombuffer(h.GetArray(), dtype=np.float64, count=(n + 2) * (n + 2)).reshape(n + 2, n + 2)
    42	    C = np.ascontiguousarray(arr[1:n + 1, 1:n + 1]); f.Close(); return C
    43	
    44	
    45	def _flat(path, key):
    46	    import ROOT
    47	    f = ROOT.TFile.Open(path); h = f.Get(key)
    48	    if not h:
    49	        f.Close(); raise P.P4GateError(f"missing {key} in {path}")
    50	    v = np.array([h.GetBinContent(i + 1) for i in range(h.GetNbinsX())]); f.Close(); return v
    51	
    52	
    53	def _band_keys(path):
    54	    import ROOT
    55	    f = ROOT.TFile.Open(path)
    56	    ks = [k.GetName() for k in f.GetListOfKeys()]; f.Close()
    57	    return sorted(k[len("hCov_universe5d_"):] for k in ks
    58	                  if k.startswith("hCov_universe5d_") and k != "hCov_universe5d_total")
    59	
    60	
    61	def _chash(C):
    62	    return P.hashlib.sha256(np.ascontiguousarray(C, dtype=np.float64).tobytes()).hexdigest()
    63	
    64	
    65	def build_active_bands(manifest, idx):
    66	    UDIR = "active_universe_5d/standard/unfolds"
    67	    bands = {}
    68	    for b in P.BANDS:
    69	        pair = []
    70	        for ep in P.ENDPOINTS:
    71	            tag = f"{b}_{ep}"; p = f"{UDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_{tag}.root"
    72	            P.require(P.sha256_file(p) == manifest["endpoint_sha256"][tag], f"endpoint {tag} sha256 drift")
    73	            pair.append(_flat(p, "hXSecND_flat")[idx])
    74	        bands[b] = mat_covariance(np.stack([pair[0], pair[1]]))   # MAT biased 1/N (preserved)
    75	    P.require_exact_bands(bands); P.component_traces_positive_finite(bands)
    76	    return bands
    77	
    78	
    79	def main():
    80	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    81	    ap.add_argument("--manifest", required=True)

    98	    hmask, nmask = P.mask_order_hash(mask)

   100	    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]

   114	    syst_sum = np.zeros_like(Csyst_total)

   116	        syst_sum = syst_sum + comp[b]

   121	    P.prove_identity(Ccomb_total, Csyst_total + C_stat + C_ml, 1e-6, "C_combined == C_syst + C_stat + C_ML")
   122	
   123	    # --- pure-addition candidate ---
   124	    active = build_active_bands(man, idx)

   129	    Csyst_active = np.zeros_like(Csyst_total)
   130	    for b in retained:
   131	        Csyst_active = Csyst_active + comp[b]
   132	    Csyst_active = Csyst_active + active_only
   133	    Ccomb_active = Csyst_active + C_stat + C_ml
   134	    # identity gates: active-only == sum(5 active); full == retained + active + stat + ml
   135	    _err_active5 = P.check_component_sum(active_only, active)           # exactly 5 bands
   136	    full_check = sum(comp[b] for b in retained) + active_only + C_stat + C_ml
   137	    _err_full = P.prove_identity(Ccomb_active, full_check, 1e-9,

   139	    # repair-5 (pattern sweep): also prove the identity a DOWNSTREAM reader can re-derive from

   142	    _err_syst = P.prove_identity(Csyst_active,

   145	    _err_fulltotal = P.check_full_total_identity(Ccomb_active, Csyst_active, C_stat, C_ml, 1e-9)

   156	            # repair-5 (pattern sweep: "assert presence, never compare"). These four were
   157	            # written as LITERAL `True` -- the proofs ran just above, but the recorded values
   158	            # were constants, so a consumer reading the manifest learned nothing. Two consumers
   159	            # then tested `pure_addition` for truthiness (p4_validate_active_lateral.py and
   160	            # p4_adopt_standard.py), i.e. read a literal and treated it as evidence: a closed
   161	            # loop of self-assertion, the same shape as the P4_VERIFIER_PASS non-emptiness gate
   162	            # (KNOWN_ISSUES #21). Now each records the MEASURED max-relative error from the

   164	            "identities": {"active_only_eq_sum5_relerr": float(_err_active5),

   171	    # repair-4 (defect 4b): the manifest used to be written HERE, before the candidate ROOT
   172	    # existed and with no candidate hash in it -- so a manifest could describe a candidate that

   175	    # discipline the unfold launcher already uses.
   176	    import ROOT
   177	    n = Ccomb_active.shape[0]; fo = ROOT.TFile.Open(a.out, "RECREATE")
   178	    def wr(name, C, title):
   179	        h = ROOT.TH2D(name, title, n, 0, n, n, 0, n)
   180	        h.SetContent(np.ascontiguousarray(np.pad(C, 1)[0:n + 2, 0:n + 2], dtype=np.float64).ravel())
   181	        h.Write()
   182	    # repair-4 (D4a): the candidate persisted only the five active blocks and three totals, so

   187	    for b in retained:
   188	        wr(f"hCov_retained5d_{b}", comp[b], f"retained non-lateral component {b}")
   189	    # repair-4 (D1c): key names come from p4_lib so the driver cannot name a key nothing writes
   190	    for b in P.BANDS:
   191	        wr(P.candidate_band_key(b), active[b], f"active MAT band {b}")
   192	    wr(P.CANDIDATE_ACTIVE_TOTAL_KEY, active_only, "active-only total (sum of 5 MAT bands)")
   193	    wr(P.CANDIDATE_SYST_KEY, Csyst_active, "candidate C_syst (retained + active)")
   194	    wr(P.CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY, Ccomb_active, "candidate full total (C_syst + stat + ML)")
   195	    fo.Close()
   196	    # candidate is now published; bind it into the manifest and write that LAST
   197	    written_keys = ([f"hCov_retained5d_{b}" for b in retained]
   198	                    + [P.candidate_band_key(b) for b in P.BANDS]
   199	                    + [P.CANDIDATE_ACTIVE_TOTAL_KEY, P.CANDIDATE_SYST_KEY, P.CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY])
   200	    prov["candidate"] = os.path.abspath(a.out)
   201	    prov["candidate_sha256"] = P.sha256_file(a.out)
   202	    prov["candidate_keys"] = written_keys
   203	    prov["candidate_total_key"] = P.CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY
   204	    tmp_manifest = a.out_manifest + ".tmp"
   205	    json.dump(prov, open(tmp_manifest, "w"), indent=2)
   206	    os.replace(tmp_manifest, a.out_manifest)        # atomic; manifest-last
   207	    print(f"CANDIDATE {a.out}: sqrt_tr_syst={np.sqrt(max(0,np.trace(Csyst_active))):.4e} "
   208	          f"sqrt_tr_full={np.sqrt(max(0,np.trace(Ccomb_active))):.4e} bands={len(all_bands)} "
   209	          f"retained={len(retained)}; prov -> {a.out_manifest}")
   210	    sys.exit(0)
   211	
   212	
   213	if __name__ == "__main__":
   214	    try:
   215	        main()
   216	    except P.P4GateError as e:
   217	        print(f"FAIL-CLOSED :: {e}"); sys.exit(1)

    20	def _th2(path, key):

    14	import numpy as np

    17	CEN5 = "products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root"

    23	def _flat(path, key="hXSecND_flat"):
    24	    import ROOT
    25	    f = ROOT.TFile.Open(path); h = f.Get(key)
    26	    v = np.array([h.GetBinContent(i + 1) for i in range(h.GetNbinsX())]); f.Close(); return v
    27	
    28	
    29	def _th2(path, key):
    30	    import ROOT
    31	    f = ROOT.TFile.Open(path); h = f.Get(key)
    32	    n = h.GetNbinsX()

    34	    C = np.ascontiguousarray(arr[1:n + 1, 1:n + 1]); f.Close(); return C

    37	def canonical_edges():
    38	    """Ordered 5-axis edges (pt,pz,eavail,q3,W) from the canonical source."""
    39	    from project_cov_nd import AXIS_EDGES  # canonical, drift-guarded mirror
    40	    order = ["pt", "pz", "eavail", "q3", "W"]
    41	    return [np.asarray(AXIS_EDGES[k], float) for k in order]

    45	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()

     2	"""P4 standard LATER-ONLY adoption CLI — FAIL-CLOSED (repair round 3, 2026-07-18).

     7	candidate validator PASS receipt; and rejects aliasing (out path == any input) or any

    10	"""

    12	import p4_lib as P
    13	
    14	
    15	def main():

    18	    ap.add_argument("--component-manifest", required=True)

    20	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, help="adopted product path")
    21	    ap.add_argument("--i-understand-adoption", action="store_true")
    22	    a = ap.parse_args()
    23	
    24	    P.require(a.i_understand_adoption, "adoption requires explicit --i-understand-adoption")
    25	    prov = json.load(open(a.component_manifest))
    26	    val = json.load(open(a.validation))
    27	    P.require(val.get("result") == "PASS", "candidate validator did not PASS")
    28	    # identities: the expected vertical/unified inputs are present and hash-matched

    30	                      ("stat_sha256", prov["stat_cov"].split(":")[0]),
    31	                      ("ml_sha256", prov["ml_cov"].split(":")[0])):

    33	        P.require(P.sha256_file(path) == prov[key], f"input identity drift: {path}")
    34	    # repair-5 (pattern sweep): this read `identities.pure_addition` and tested it for

    36	    # the chain was reading a constant and calling it evidence. The builder now records MEASURED
    37	    # errors; this consumer checks them against the declared tolerance instead of a boolean.
    38	    _ids = prov.get("identities", {})
    39	    P.require(isinstance(_ids, dict) and _ids, "component manifest records no identities")

    41	              "component manifest carries the retired self-asserted `pure_addition` flag; it "
    42	              "predates repair-5, when the identities were literals rather than measurements")

    48	                  f"component manifest records a FAILING identity {_k}={_ids[_k]} > {_rtol}")

    50	    reals = {os.path.realpath(p) for p in (a.candidate, prov["support_family"],

    52	    P.require(os.path.realpath(a.out) not in reals, "aliasing: --out coincides with an input/candidate")

    54	    # file was readable. It bound nothing. Now that the validator's receipt records which candidate

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Mutation harness for the repair-4/5 self-guards (repair-6 item 3).
     3	
     4	**The rule this file enforces:** a self-guard must FAIL against the pre-repair code, and that
     5	failure must be DEMONSTRATED, not asserted. Verifier finding 3 on repair-5 was that at least
     6	one guard shared an assumption with the code it guarded — the D2 integration stub set the
     7	"live" blob equal to its own fixture, which is exactly the configuration in which the defect it
     8	was written for is invisible. If one guard had that shape, others may.
     9	
    10	**How it works.** For each repaired defect, reintroduce the defect — either by calling the
    11	guard with pre-repair inputs, or by applying the pre-repair logic to a copy of the module —
    12	and assert the guard rejects it. A guard that still passes under mutation does not discriminate
    13	and is recorded here as such rather than being quietly kept.
    14	
    15	**What this deliberately does NOT do:** assert on source text. Every case below runs the real
    16	gate. Source-text assertions live in the other suites and are explicitly *weaker* evidence;
    17	where a guard could only be expressed textually, it is listed in
    18	`NON_DISCRIMINATING` with the reason.
    19	"""
    20	import importlib.util, json, os, subprocess, sys, tempfile, unittest
    21	from pathlib import Path
    22	
    23	ND = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    24	sys.path.insert(0, str(ND))
    25	import numpy as np
    26	import p4_lib as P
    27	from p4_lib import P4GateError
    28	
    29	
    30	def _load_mutated(module_name, replacements):
    31	    """Import a private copy of a module with `replacements` [(old, new)] applied to its source.
    32	    This is how the PRE-REPAIR behaviour is reconstructed without touching the repo."""
    33	    src = (ND / module_name).read_text()
    34	    for old, new in replacements:
    35	        assert old in src, f"mutation anchor not found in {module_name}: {old[:60]!r}"
    36	        src = src.replace(old, new)
    37	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    38	        p = Path(td) / module_name
    39	        p.write_text(src)
    40	        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(f"_mut_{module_name[:-3]}", p)
    41	        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    42	        sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
    43	        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    44	        return mod
    45

   173	class BEN044_AbsoluteToleranceAtRealScale(unittest.TestCase):
   174	    """REPAIR-6, folding in the PET lane's BEN-044 ("an absolute tolerance inherited into a
   175	    problem whose natural scale is ~1e-80 makes a gate that cannot fail").
   176	
   177	    **Honest severity, because this thread is about not overclaiming.** `check_symmetric_psd`
   178	    did contain a bare absolute literal -- `d >= -1e-30` -- against a standard-5D covariance
   179	    whose diagonal sits near 1e-79, i.e. ~49 orders above what it bounds, so that specific
   180	    bound could not fire. But it was **redundant, not the sole line of defence**: for a
   181	    symmetric matrix min(diag) >= min(eigenvalue), so any negative diagonal is already a
   182	    negative eigenvalue, and the PSD check immediately above it IS relative
   183	    (`ev[0] >= -psd_atol_ratio * |ev[-1]|`) and rejects the same corruption first. The tests
   184	    below demonstrate exactly that, rather than claiming a live hole.
   185	
   186	    So the fix is hygiene under BEN-044 rule 1 (no bare literal in a covariance path) plus a
   187	    reintroduction guard -- not the closing of an exploitable gap. Stating it the other way
   188	    would be the overclaim this round exists to stop."""
   189	
   190	    SCALE = 1e-79            # realistic standard-5D diagonal magnitude
   191	
   192	    def _cov(self, n=4):
   193	        return np.diag(np.full(n, self.SCALE))
   194	
   195	    def test_gate_accepts_a_clean_covariance_at_the_real_scale(self):
   196	        st = P.check_symmetric_psd(self._cov())
   197	        self.assertGreater(st["max_eig"], 0.0)
   198	
   199	    def test_the_old_absolute_diagonal_bound_could_not_fire_at_this_scale(self):
   200	        """The literal itself: a corruption ~48 orders larger than the signal passes it."""

   244	class ReproducibilityTolerance(unittest.TestCase):
   245	    """REPAIR-6: the declared reproducibility SPECIFICATION (Joseph, 2026-08-07).
   246	
   247	    These ROOTs are not bit-reproducible, so the gate compares CONTENTS. The declared
   248	    tolerances are ~2 orders above the measured floor so a CONC change does not force a
   249	    re-derivation. This is a specification, not a loosening -- nothing was failing when it was
   250	    set, and the floor was measured on a clean 10/10 run.
   251	
   252	    The interesting property, and the reason there are TWO tolerances rather than one: together
   253	    they separate round-off from a coherent shift. The per-bin leg permits scatter; the integral
   254	    leg forbids that scatter from summing coherently."""
   255	
   256	    def setUp(self):
   257	        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
   258	        self.base = self.rng.uniform(1, 10, 10694)     # one endpoint's reported bins
   259	
   260	    def test_declared_values_are_above_the_measured_floor(self):
   261	        f = P.REPRO_MEASURED_FLOOR
   262	        self.assertGreater(P.REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN, f["worst_rel_bin"])
   263	        self.assertGreater(P.REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL, f["integral_rel"])
   264	        # and recorded separately, so re-measuring cannot silently move the gate
   265	        self.assertNotEqual(P.REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN, f["worst_rel_bin"])
   266	
   267	    def test_accepts_round_off_at_the_measured_floor(self):
   268	        """A gate that cannot PASS on real input is unverified in the direction that matters."""
   269	        s = self.rng.normal(0, 4e-12, self.base.size); s -= s.mean()
   270	        r = P.check_reproducibility(self.base * (1 + s), self.base)
   271	        self.assertLess(r["max_rel_bin"], P.REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN)
   272	
   273	    def test_rejects_a_coherent_shift_of_the_SAME_per_bin_magnitude(self):
   274	        """THE discriminating case. A uniform 2e-11 shift has a per-bin size inside the floor,
   275	        so the per-bin leg alone would accept it; the integral leg rejects it. This is what the
   276	        sign argument was mistakenly asked to do, and what magnitude+coherence actually does."""
   277	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError) as cm:
   278	            P.check_reproducibility(self.base * (1 + 2e-11), self.base)
   279	        self.assertIn("integral relative difference", str(cm.exception))
   280	
   281	    def test_rejects_a_gross_shift_on_the_per_bin_leg(self):
   282	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError) as cm:
   283	            P.check_reproducibility(self.base * (1 + 1e-3), self.base)
   284	        self.assertIn("per-bin relative difference", str(cm.exception))
   285	
   286	    def test_shape_mismatch_and_empty_reference_fail_closed(self):
   287	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):
   288	            P.check_reproducibility(self.base[:-1], self.base)
   289	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):
   290	            P.check_reproducibility(np.zeros(4), np.zeros(4))

   293	class A1_VerifierTokenBinding(unittest.TestCase):

   295	    'assert presence, never compare', and the one left open through repairs 4 and 5 because it
   296	    was the instrument authorising those rounds.

   315	                            "the pre-repair gate accepted any non-empty string -- the defect")

   337	                continue

   339	                continue

   345	                            or "authorizes_covariance_stages_4_6" in msg, msg)
   346	        self.assertGreater(found, 0, "expected at least one committed BLOCK verdict to test")

   364	class REPAIR6b_CodeRevReachability(unittest.TestCase):
   365	    """REPAIR-6b: the D2 code_rev check was too strict and broke on CORRECT behaviour.
   366	
   367	    Requiring `code_rev == HEAD` expired all ten receipts the moment ANY commit landed
   368	    anywhere in the repo -- including the PET lane's, touching nothing this chain reads. Caught
   369	    on the first production run: HEAD moved 42268b6 -> 203ff01 while `git diff` on the unfold
   370	    driver was EMPTY, and the gate rejected 10/10 valid receipts.
   371	
   372	    That is the same defect KNOWN_ISSUES #24 names -- a chain that breaks on correct behaviour
   373	    is a defect in the chain. The producing-code binding is `unfold_blob` (still strict); the
   374	    honest check on `code_rev` is REACHABILITY."""
   375	
   376	    import subprocess as _sp
   377	
   378	    def _head(self):
   379	        return self._sp.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
   380	                                     cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True).strip()
   381	
   382	    def _older(self, n=3):
   383	        return self._sp.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"HEAD~{n}"],
   384	                                     cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True).strip()
   385	
   386	    def test_an_older_in_history_commit_is_accepted(self):
   387	        """The case that broke: a receipt produced before unrelated commits landed."""
   388	        self.assertTrue(P.code_rev_in_history(self._older()))
   389	
   390	    def test_head_itself_is_accepted(self):
   391	        self.assertTrue(P.code_rev_in_history(self._head()))
   392	
   393	    def test_a_commit_not_in_this_history_is_rejected(self):
   394	        self.assertFalse(P.code_rev_in_history("0" * 40))
   395	
   396	    def test_empty_and_garbage_fail_closed(self):
   397	        for bad in ("", "   ", None, 12345, "not-a-sha"):
   398	            self.assertFalse(P.code_rev_in_history(bad), f"{bad!r} accepted")
   399	
   400	    def test_MUTATION_equality_would_reject_a_valid_older_receipt(self):

   420	class REPAIR6b_SelfCheckPipelineBug(unittest.TestCase):
   421	    """REPAIR-6b: my own self-check reported `pass=10 fail=0` beside ten REJECT lines.
   422	
   423	    `if cmd | tail -1; then pass++` tests TAIL's exit status, never the command's -- BEN-035's
   424	    'rc after a pipeline' trap, in the script written to catch exactly this family of defect."""
   425	
   426	    def test_the_pipeline_idiom_is_the_bug(self):
   427	        import subprocess
   428	        rc = subprocess.call("if false | tail -1; then exit 0; else exit 1; fi", shell=True)
   429	        self.assertEqual(rc, 0, "a failing command piped to tail reports SUCCESS")
   430	
   431	    def test_capture_then_test_is_correct(self):
   432	        import subprocess
   433	        rc = subprocess.call('if OUT=$(false); then exit 0; else exit 1; fi', shell=True)
   434	        self.assertEqual(rc, 1, "capturing first preserves the command's status")

   437	class REPAIR6c_TokenGateReviewScope(unittest.TestCase):
   438	    """REPAIR-6c: the token gate carried the SAME spec flaw as the receipt gate.
   439	
   440	    Rule (4) required the verdict's `code_rev == HEAD`. Another lane pushing between the PASS and
   441	    stages 4-6 -- eight commits, today -- would have rejected a valid token over commits touching
   442	    nothing the verifier reviewed, wasting the delegate run. Replaced by ancestry PLUS
   443	    reviewed-files-unchanged, which is strictly stronger: it checks what the rule protects
   444	    instead of a proxy unrelated commits perturb."""
   445	
   446	    import subprocess as _sp
   447	
   448	    def _rev(self, spec="HEAD"):
   449	        return self._sp.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", spec],
   450	                                     cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True).strip()
   451	
   452	    def test_review_surface_resolves_to_real_tracked_files(self):
   453	        files = P.tracked_files_matching(P.STANDARD_P4_SURFACE_GLOBS)
   454	        self.assertGreater(len(files), 5)
   455	        self.assertTrue(all(f.startswith("nd-unfolding/") for f in files))
   456	        self.assertIn("nd-unfolding/p4_lib.py", files)
   457	
   458	    def test_unchanged_between_identical_revs_is_true(self):
   459	        files = P.tracked_files_matching(P.STANDARD_P4_SURFACE_GLOBS)
   460	        ok, diff = P.paths_unchanged_between("HEAD", "HEAD", files)
   461	        self.assertTrue(ok); self.assertEqual(diff, [])
   462	
   463	    def test_changed_reviewed_file_is_detected(self):
   464	        """The property the rule exists to protect: a PASS must not authorize unseen code.
   465	
   466	        Constructed DETERMINISTICALLY rather than from `HEAD~1`. The first version assumed the
   467	        previous commit touched the P4 surface, which is a fact about repo history, not about
   468	        the property under test -- it went red the moment a merge from another lane became
   469	        HEAD~1. A test whose premise depends on what someone else committed is a flaky test."""
   470	        target = "nd-unfolding/p4_lib.py"
   471	        # the last commit that actually changed p4_lib.py, and its parent
   472	        rev = self._sp.check_output(["git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H", "--", target],
   473	                                    cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True).strip()
   474	        parent = self._sp.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", f"{rev}^"],
   475	                                       cwd=P.REPO_ROOT, text=True).strip()
   476	        ok, diff = P.paths_unchanged_between(parent, rev, [target])
   477	        self.assertFalse(ok, f"{target} changed in {rev[:8]}; that must be visible")
   478	        self.assertEqual(diff, [target])
   479	
   480	    def test_unresolvable_path_fails_closed(self):
   481	        ok, diff = P.paths_unchanged_between("HEAD", "HEAD", ["nd-unfolding/does_not_exist.py"])
   482	        self.assertFalse(ok, "an unverifiable path must count as differing, not as satisfied")
   483	
   484	    def test_MUTATION_equality_would_reject_after_an_unrelated_push(self):
   485	        """The defect, shown: HEAD~1 != HEAD, so equality rejects a verdict reviewed one commit
   486	        ago even when nothing it reviewed changed."""
   487	        older, head = self._rev("HEAD~1"), self._rev("HEAD")
   488	        self.assertNotEqual(older, head)
   489	        self.assertTrue(P.code_rev_in_history(older),
   490	                        "ancestry accepts it; equality did not -- that was the defect")
   491	
   492	    def test_token_gate_uses_ancestry_not_equality(self):
   493	        src = (ND / "p4_check_verifier_token.py").read_text()
   494	        code = "\n".join(l for l in src.splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith("#"))
   495	        self.assertIn("code_rev_in_history", code)
   496	        self.assertIn("paths_unchanged_between", code)
   497	        self.assertNotIn('head.startswith(str(cr))', code)   # the retired equality
   498

   500	class REPAIR6c_WidenedIntegralTolerance(unittest.TestCase):
   501	    """REPAIR-6c: REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL widened 1e-12 -> 1e-11 from measured spread.
   502	
   503	    Specification, not loosening: nothing was failing. The per-bin leg had 52x margin while the
   504	    integral leg had 3.2x and its two observations spanned 12x -- set up to false-alarm on a
   505	    third run."""
   506	
   507	    def test_declared_values(self):
   508	        self.assertEqual(P.REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN, 1e-9)
   509	        self.assertEqual(P.REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL, 1e-11)
   510	
   511	    def test_both_legs_now_carry_comparable_margin(self):
   512	        f = P.REPRO_MEASURED_FLOOR
   513	        bin_margin = P.REPRO_RTOL_PER_BIN / f["worst_rel_bin"]
   514	        int_margin = P.REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL / 3.14e-13      # worst observed integral
   515	        self.assertGreater(bin_margin, 20); self.assertGreater(int_margin, 20)
   516	        self.assertLess(max(bin_margin, int_margin) / min(bin_margin, int_margin), 4.0,
   517	                        "the two legs should be within a small factor of each other")
   518	
   519	    def test_measured_floor_was_NOT_moved_with_the_spec(self):
   520	        """The whole point of recording them separately."""
   521	        f = P.REPRO_MEASURED_FLOOR
   522	        self.assertEqual(f["worst_rel_bin"], 1.9e-11)
   523	        self.assertEqual(f["integral_rel"], 2.6e-14)
   524	        self.assertNotEqual(f["integral_rel"], P.REPRO_RTOL_INTEGRAL)
   525	
   526	    def test_discrimination_survives_the_widening(self):
   527	        """A uniform shift between 1e-11 and 1e-9 must still pass per-bin and FAIL integral."""
   528	        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
   529	        base = rng.uniform(1, 10, 4096)
   530	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError) as cm:
   531	            P.check_reproducibility(base * (1 + 5e-11), base)
   532	        self.assertIn("integral relative difference", str(cm.exception))
   533	        # and real scatter at the measured floor still passes
   534	        sc = rng.normal(0, 4e-12, base.size); sc -= sc.mean()
   535	        self.assertIsInstance(P.check_reproducibility(base * (1 + sc), base), dict)
   536	
   537	
   538	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   539	# Guards that could NOT be made discriminating, recorded rather than quietly kept.
   540	NON_DISCRIMINATING = {
   541	    "launcher wiring assertions (test_p4_resume_integration.LauncherWiring)":
   542	        "assert on shell source text. Executing run_p4_unfold_std.sh needs PyROOT, 538 GB of "
   543	        "merged input and Slurm, so there is no laptop-side execution to mutate against. "
   544	        "Verifier finding 3 is correct that these are weak; they are retained as drift "
   545	        "detectors, not as evidence the launcher behaves correctly.",
   546	    "D2 integration stub (ReceiptGateIntegration)":
   547	        "stubs committed_unfold_blob()/current_code_rev() to equal its fixture, so it cannot "
   548	        "expose a mis-stamped LEGACY receipt. Repair-6 removes the legacy-attest path entirely "
   549	        "rather than guarding it, which is why this stub is acceptable going forward: there is "
   550	        "no longer a code path that stamps provenance it did not observe.",
   551	}
   552

   457	class Repair4DriverContract(unittest.TestCase):
   458	    """REPAIR-4, verifier defect 1. Stages 4-6 had NEVER executed, so nothing caught that the
   459	    driver called the validator and projector with arguments they do not define and a ROOT key
   460	    nothing writes. These tests derive the expectation from the REAL callees -- each script's
   461	    own argparse and the builder's own key constants -- so they fail if either side drifts.
   462	    That is the point: the previous suite asserted only `rc != 0` and was satisfied by an
   463	    argparse error (defect 6b)."""
   464	
   465	    ND = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
   466	
   467	    def _driver(self):
   468	        return (self.ND / "run_p4_standard.sh").read_text()
   469	
   470	    def _argparse_opts(self, script):
   471	        """The set of long options a script actually defines, read from its source."""
   472	        import re
   473	        src = (self.ND / script).read_text()
   474	        return set(re.findall(r'add_argument\("(--[a-z0-9-]+)"', src))
   475	
   476	    def _driver_opts_for(self, script):
   477	        """The long options the driver passes to `script`, read from the driver."""
   478	        import re
   479	        drv = self._driver()
   480	        m = re.search(rf"python3 {re.escape(script)}((?:[^\n]*\\\n)*[^\n]*)", drv)
   481	        self.assertIsNotNone(m, f"driver does not invoke {script}")
   482	        return set(re.findall(r"(--[a-z0-9-]+)", m.group(1)))
   483	
   484	    # ---------- D1b/D1c: the driver must speak each callee's real CLI ----------
   485	    def test_driver_passes_only_options_the_validator_defines(self):
   486	        passed = self._driver_opts_for("p4_validate_active_lateral.py")
   487	        defined = self._argparse_opts("p4_validate_active_lateral.py")
   488	        self.assertTrue(passed, "no options parsed from the validator invocation")
   489	        self.assertEqual(passed - defined, set(),
   490	                         f"driver passes options the validator does not define: {passed - defined}")
   491	
   492	    def test_driver_supplies_every_required_validator_option(self):
   493	        passed = self._driver_opts_for("p4_validate_active_lateral.py")
   494	        for req in ("--candidate", "--support", "--manifest", "--merged-audit", "--out"):
   495	            self.assertIn(req, passed, f"driver omits required validator option {req}")
   496	
   497	    def test_driver_passes_only_options_the_projector_defines(self):
   498	        passed = self._driver_opts_for("p4_project_4d.py")
   499	        defined = self._argparse_opts("p4_project_4d.py")
   500	        self.assertEqual(passed - defined, set(),
   501	                         f"driver passes options the projector does not define: {passed - defined}")
   502	        self.assertNotIn("--proj", passed)        # the exact retired argument from defect 1
   503	
   504	    def test_driver_passes_only_options_the_builder_defines(self):
   505	        passed = self._driver_opts_for("p4_build_components.py")
   506	        defined = self._argparse_opts("p4_build_components.py")
   507	        self.assertEqual(passed - defined, set(),
   508	                         f"driver passes options the builder does not define: {passed - defined}")
   509	
   510	    # ---------- D1c: the key must be one the builder actually writes ----------
   511	    DEAD_KEY = "hCov_std" + "_final5_candidate"      # split so this file is not its own hit
   512	
   513	    @staticmethod
   514	    def _code_lines(text):
   515	        """Lines that are not wholly a `#` comment. Keeps history in comments testable-around:
   516	        documenting the dead key is good, *referencing* it is the defect."""
   517	        return [l for l in text.splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith("#")]
   518	
   519	    def test_candidate_key_is_produced_by_the_builder(self):
   520	        builder = (self.ND / "p4_build_components.py").read_text()
   521	        self.assertIn("P.CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY", builder)      # builder writes the shared constant
   522	        self.assertIn("CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY", self._driver()) # driver reads the same one
   523	        self.assertNotIn(self.DEAD_KEY, "\n".join(self._code_lines(self._driver())))
   524	
   525	    def test_dead_candidate_key_appears_in_no_executable_line(self):
   526	        import subprocess
   527	        r = subprocess.run(["git", "grep", "-l", self.DEAD_KEY],
   528	                           cwd=self.ND.parent, capture_output=True, text=True)
   529	        offenders = []
   530	        for rel in r.stdout.splitlines():
   531	            if not rel.strip() or rel.endswith(".md") or "runs/" in rel or "tests/" in rel:
   532	                continue
   533	            body = (self.ND.parent / rel).read_text(errors="replace")
   534	            if self.DEAD_KEY in "\n".join(self._code_lines(body)):
   535	                offenders.append(rel)
   536	        self.assertEqual(offenders, [],
   537	                         f"dead candidate key referenced in executable code: {offenders}")
   538	
   539	    def test_candidate_keys_are_single_sourced(self):
   540	        self.assertEqual(P.CANDIDATE_TOTAL_KEY, "hCov_stdcombined5d_total_candidate")
   541	        self.assertEqual(P.CANDIDATE_SYST_KEY, "hCov_stdsyst5d_total_candidate")
   542	        self.assertEqual(P.candidate_band_key("BeamAngleX"), "hCov_active5d_BeamAngleX")
   543	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):
   544	            P.candidate_band_key("NotABand")
   545	
   546	    # ---------- D1a: stage order ----------
   547	    def test_stage_order_is_audit_then_unfold_then_evidence(self):
   548	        drv = self._driver()
   549	        i_audit = drv.index("run bash run_p4_merge_audit_std.sh")
   550	        i_unfold = drv.index("run bash run_p4_unfold_std.sh")
   551	        i_evid = drv.index("python3 p4_evidence.py")
   552	        self.assertLess(i_audit, i_unfold, "merge+audit must precede unfold")
   553	        self.assertLess(i_unfold, i_evid,
   554	                        "endpoint evidence must come AFTER unfold, or the manifest describes "
   555	                        "endpoints the next stage can rewrite")
   556	
   557	    def test_default_stop_after_is_the_safe_preflight(self):
   558	        drv = self._driver()
   559	        self.assertIn('STOP_AFTER="${STOP_AFTER:-audit}"', drv)
   560	        self.assertIn("unknown STOP_AFTER", drv)          # invalid values abort, not fall through
   561	
   562	    def test_covariance_stages_are_still_gated(self):
   563	        """Compare EXECUTABLE positions, not the header comment -- both strings appear in the
   564	        stage-list comment at the top, so a naive index() compares documentation."""
   565	        code = "\n".join(self._code_lines(self._driver()))
   566	        self.assertIn("P4_VERIFIER_PASS", code)
   567	        self.assertLess(code.index("P4_VERIFIER_PASS"),
   568	                        code.index("python3 p4_build_components.py"),
   569	                        "the verifier gate must precede component construction")

   737	            self.assertIn(tok, src)

   740	class Repair5SelfGuards(unittest.TestCase):
   741	    """REPAIR-5. One assertion per defect that FAILS if the defect is reintroduced.
   742	
   743	    Every check here is a live computation, not a source-text grep, except where the thing
   744	    being guarded IS a source property. If any of these can be deleted and the four defects
   745	    still stay fixed, the defect was not really closed."""
   746	
   747	    ND = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
   748	
   749	    # ---------- D3: dirty-source guard must fail closed on a DELETED source ----------
   750	    def test_deleted_bound_source_is_a_blocker_not_a_pass(self):
   751	        """The old guard read `_w is None or _c == _w`; _worktree_blob returns None when the
   752	        file is gone, so DELETING a bound source passed the dirty check."""
   753	        src = (self.ND / "p4_evidence.py").read_text()
   754	        code = "\n".join(l for l in src.splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith("#"))
   755	        self.assertNotIn("_w is None or _c == _w", code)   # the fail-OPEN disjunct is gone
   756	        self.assertIn("need(_c == _w,", code)              # replaced by a direct comparison
   757	        self.assertIn("ABSENT from the working tree", code)
   758	        self.assertIn("os.path.exists(_abs)", code)
   759	        self.assertIn("`git hash-object` failed", code)   # git-unavailable distinguished
   760	
   761	    # ---------- D4a: containment must survive a symlink escape ----------
   762	    def test_symlink_escape_is_rejected(self):
   763	        """A symlink inside the candidate dir pointing out of it must not launder a path.
   764	        normpath could not see this; realpath can."""
   765	        import tempfile, os
   766	        cand_root = os.path.join(P.ND_ROOT, P.CANDIDATE_SUBDIR)
   767	        os.makedirs(cand_root, exist_ok=True)
   768	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as outside:
   769	            link = os.path.join(cand_root, "_p5_escape_probe")
   770	            if os.path.islink(link) or os.path.exists(link):
   771	                os.remove(link)
   772	            os.symlink(outside, link)
   773	            try:
   774	                with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):
   775	                    P.require_candidate_path(os.path.join(link, "out.root"))
   776	            finally:
   777	                os.remove(link)

   779	    def test_absolute_path_outside_the_repo_is_rejected(self):
   780	        """The verifier's exact bypass: the component sequence appearing anywhere."""
   781	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):

   792	    # ---------- D4b: PSD is not the identity ----------
   793	    def test_psd_residual_that_is_not_stat_plus_ml_fails(self):
   794	        """THE self-guard for the overclaim. This residual is symmetric PSD -- so it passes the
   795	        repair-4 check -- but it is NOT stat+ML, so the real identity must reject it."""
   796	        Csyst = np.diag([4.0, 9.0])
   797	        Cstat = np.diag([1.0, 1.0])
   798	        Cml = np.diag([0.5, 0.5])
   799	        Ccomb_good = Csyst + Cstat + Cml
   800	        self.assertLessEqual(
   801	            P.check_full_total_identity(Ccomb_good, Csyst, Cstat, Cml, 1e-9), 1e-9)
   802	        # residual = diag(3,3): symmetric, PSD, and wrong
   803	        Ccomb_bad = Csyst + np.diag([3.0, 3.0])
   804	        P.check_symmetric_psd(Ccomb_bad - Csyst)          # the weak check still passes...
   805	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):              # ...the real identity does not
   806	            P.check_full_total_identity(Ccomb_bad, Csyst, Cstat, Cml, 1e-9)
   807	
   808	    def test_full_total_identity_catches_a_swapped_stat_block(self):
   809	        Csyst = np.diag([4.0, 9.0]); Cstat = np.diag([1.0, 2.0]); Cml = np.diag([0.5, 0.5])
   810	        Ccomb = Csyst + Cstat + Cml
   811	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):
   812	            P.check_full_total_identity(Ccomb, Csyst, np.diag([2.0, 1.0]), Cml, 1e-9)
   813

   825	                "active-universe selection-complete", 0, "BeamAngleX", nz, zs)
   826	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):      # declared bin-only but migrated
   827	            P.check_declared_migration_policy("bin-migration only", 17, "MuonResolution", nz, zs)
   828	        with self.assertRaises(P4GateError):      # migrates but policy does not claim it
   829	            P.check_declared_migration_policy("weights only", 4792, "BeamAngleX", nz, zs)

   831	            P.check_declared_migration_policy("", 0, "MuonResolution", nz, zs)

   896	class Repair4ReceiptSchema(unittest.TestCase):
   897	    """REPAIR-4, verifier defect 2. The resume path accepted any ROOT plus any nonempty .done.
   898	
   899	    The receipt fixture here is built by running the launcher's OWN `printf` format string,
   900	    extracted from run_p4_unfold_std.sh at test time -- not hand-written to match the reader.
   901	    If the producer's field list changes, this fixture changes with it, and a reader that
   902	    drifted from the writer fails here. That inversion (fixture shaped like the consumer) is
   903	    precisely BEN-040, in this same chain."""
   904	
   905	    ND = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
   906	
   907	    # repair-6b: code_rev is checked for REACHABILITY in this history, so the fixture uses the
   908	    # real HEAD. A synthetic sha is precisely what the gate now rejects.
   909	    import subprocess as _sp
   910	    _HEAD = _sp.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
   911	                             cwd=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]), text=True).strip()
   912	    GOOD = dict(tag="BeamAngleX_0", root_sha256="a" * 64, merged_sha256="b" * 64,
   913	                central5d_sha256="c" * 64, config_hash="d" * 64, bkg_mode="purity",
   914	                code_rev=_HEAD, unfold_blob="f" * 40)
   915	
   916	    def _producer_receipt(self, mode="produced", **over):
   917	        """Render a receipt through the launcher's real format string."""
   918	        import re, subprocess, json
   919	        sh = (self.ND / "run_p4_unfold_std.sh").read_text()
   920	        m = re.search(r"printf '(\{\"tag\".*?\}\\n)'", sh, re.S)
   921	        self.assertIsNotNone(m, "could not extract the launcher's receipt format")
   922	        fmt = m.group(1)
   923	        vals = dict(self.GOOD); vals.update(over)
   924	        # positional order matches the launcher's own argument order
   925	        args = [vals["tag"], vals["root_sha256"], vals["merged_sha256"], vals["central5d_sha256"],
   926	                vals["config_hash"], vals["bkg_mode"], vals["code_rev"], vals["unfold_blob"],
   927	                "2026-08-07T00:00:00Z"]
   928	        if mode == "legacy-attested":
   929	            fmt = fmt.replace('"mode":"produced"', '"mode":"legacy-attested"')
   930	        out = subprocess.run(["printf", fmt, *args], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
   931	        return json.loads(out)
   932	
   933	    def test_producer_receipt_has_every_required_key(self):
   934	        rec = self._producer_receipt()
   935	        for k in P.RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS:
   936	            self.assertIn(k, rec, f"the real producer omits required key {k}")
   937	
   938	    def test_validator_accepts_the_real_producer_output(self):

   940	        self.assertTrue(P.validate_endpoint_receipt(rec, **self.GOOD))

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba nd-unfolding/tests/test_p4_resume_integration.py' in /Users/josephbailey/local-research/MINERvA-OmniFold/.claude/worktrees/gbdt-closeout

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""REPAIR-4, verifier defect 6a/6c — integration coverage for the resume/receipt contract.
     3	
     4	The round-3 suite executed neither shell driver, so every resume, staleness, ordering and
     5	worker-failure behaviour was untested, and the one CLI test that looked like coverage passed
     6	because argparse rejected an argument before the guard it named ever ran (defect 6b).
     7	
     8	What this file does differently:
     9	
    10	* It drives `p4_check_receipt.py` — a REAL producer/consumer pair — end to end against
    11	  receipts rendered by the LAUNCHER'S OWN `printf` format string, extracted from
    12	  `run_p4_unfold_std.sh` at test time. Nothing here is hand-assembled to match the reader
    13	  (BEN-040 was exactly that inversion, in this chain).
    14	* It asserts the SPECIFIC rejection reason, never merely a nonzero exit, so a test cannot pass
    15	  because something unrelated failed first.
    16	* It uses a fake repo layout so no real 53.8 GB input, no ROOT, and no cluster are needed;
    17	  the merged-hash lookup is stubbed at its seam rather than by rewriting the code under test.
    18	
    19	Not covered here, and deliberately: anything requiring PyROOT (`valid_root`, the builder and
    20	the projector's ROOT I/O). Those need the cluster interpreter — see KNOWN_ISSUES #17 and the
    21	Perlmutter ROOT/TF split. This file stays ROOT-free so it runs in CI and on a laptop.
    22	"""
    23	import json, os, re, subprocess, sys, tempfile, unittest
    24	from pathlib import Path
    25	
    26	ND = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    27	sys.path.insert(0, str(ND))
    28	import p4_lib as P
    29	
    30	
    31	def launcher_receipt_format():
    32	    """The produced-receipt format string, read out of the launcher itself."""
    33	    sh = (ND / "run_p4_unfold_std.sh").read_text()
    34	    m = re.search(r"printf '(\{\"tag\":\"%s\",\"mode\":\"produced\".*?\}\\n)'", sh, re.S)
    35	    assert m, "could not extract the launcher's produced-receipt format"
    36	    return m.group(1)
    37	
    38	
    39	def render_receipt(tag, root_sha, merged_sha, central_sha, cfg_hash, bkg_mode,
    40	                   code_rev, unfold_blob, when="2026-08-07T00:00:00Z"):
    41	    fmt = launcher_receipt_format()
    42	    out = subprocess.run(
    43	        ["printf", fmt, tag, root_sha, merged_sha, central_sha, cfg_hash, bkg_mode,
    44	         code_rev, unfold_blob, when],
    45	        capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    46	    return json.loads(out)
    47	
    48	
    49	class ReceiptGateIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    50	    """Executes p4_check_receipt.py as a subprocess, the way the launcher calls it."""
    51	
    52	    def setUp(self):
    53	        self.td = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
    54	        self.d = Path(self.td.name)
    55	        self.root = self.d / "endpoint.root"
    56	        self.root.write_bytes(b"pretend-root-payload")
    57	        self.central = self.d / "central.root"
    58	        self.central.write_bytes(b"pretend-central-payload")
    59	        self.merged = self.d / "merged.root"
    60	        self.merged.write_bytes(b"pretend-merged-payload")
    61	        self.root_sha = P.sha256_file(self.root)
    62	        self.central_sha = P.sha256_file(self.central)
    63	        self.merged_sha = "m" * 64
    64	        cfg = P.P4Config(); cfg.validate()
    65	        self.cfg_hash, self.bkg = cfg.hash(), cfg.bkg_mode
    66	        # repair-6b: code_rev is now checked for REACHABILITY in this repo's history, so the
    67	        # fixture must be a real commit. A synthetic sha is exactly what the gate rejects.
    68	        self.code_rev = subprocess.check_output(
    69	            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=str(ND.parent), text=True).strip()
    70	        self.unfold_blob = "u" * 40
    71	
    72	    def tearDown(self):
    73	        self.td.cleanup()
    74	
    75	    def _run_gate(self, receipt_path, tag="BeamAngleX_0"):
    76	        """Call the gate with its two external dependencies stubbed at the seam: the central
    77	        product path and the committed-merged-sha lookup. Stubbing the SEAM keeps the gate's
    78	        own logic under test, unlike patching the logic itself."""
    79	        shim = self.d / "shim.py"
    80	        shim.write_text(
    81	            "import sys\n"
    82	            f"sys.path.insert(0, {str(ND)!r})\n"
    83	            "import p4_check_receipt as C\n"
    84	            f"C.CEN5 = {str(self.central)!r}\n"
    85	            f"C.committed_merged_sha = lambda p: {self.merged_sha!r}\n"
    86	            f"C.committed_unfold_blob = lambda: {self.unfold_blob!r}\n"
    87	            f"C.current_code_rev = lambda: {self.code_rev!r}\n"
    88	            "sys.argv = ['p4_check_receipt.py', '--receipt', sys.argv[1], '--tag', sys.argv[2],"
    89	            "            '--root', sys.argv[3], '--merged', sys.argv[4]]\n"
    90	            "C.main()\n")
    91	        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(shim), str(receipt_path), tag,
    92	                            str(self.root), str(self.merged)],
    93	                           capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ND))
    94	        return r.returncode, (r.stdout + r.stderr)
    95	
    96	    def _write(self, rec, name="e.done"):
    97	        p = self.d / name
    98	        p.write_text(json.dumps(rec))
    99	        return p
   100	
   101	    def _good(self, **over):
   102	        vals = dict(tag="BeamAngleX_0", root_sha=self.root_sha, merged_sha=self.merged_sha,
   103	                    central_sha=self.central_sha, cfg_hash=self.cfg_hash, bkg_mode=self.bkg,
   104	                    code_rev=self.code_rev, unfold_blob=self.unfold_blob)
   105	        vals.update(over)
   106	        return render_receipt(**vals)
   107	
   108	    # ---------- the happy path must actually pass ----------
   109	    def test_valid_receipt_is_accepted(self):
   110	        """A gate that has never returned OK on real input is unverified in the direction that
   111	        matters (BEN-040). This is that direction."""
   112	        rc, out = self._run_gate(self._write(self._good()))
   113	        self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out)
   114	        self.assertIn("RECEIPT-OK", out)

   137	    def test_stale_code_rev_rejected_with_reason(self):
   138	        """REPAIR-5 self-guard for D2, updated by repair-6b: a code_rev that is not in this
   139	        repository's history must be rejected. (Equality with HEAD was too strict -- it expired
   140	        valid receipts on unrelated commits; see REPAIR6b_CodeRevReachability.)"""
   141	        rc, out = self._run_gate(self._write(self._good(code_rev="0" * 40)))
   142	        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)
   143	        self.assertIn("code_rev", out)
   144	        self.assertIn("not an ancestor of HEAD", out)
   145	
   146	    def test_stale_unfold_blob_rejected_with_reason(self):
   147	        """REPAIR-5 self-guard for D2. An endpoint produced by a CHANGED unfold driver must not
   148	        be skipped -- previously the receipt carried no source identity at all."""
   149	        rc, out = self._run_gate(self._write(self._good(unfold_blob="9" * 40)))
   150	        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)
   151	        self.assertIn("unfold driver changed", out)
   152	
   153	    def test_receipt_without_source_identity_rejected(self):
   154	        """The pre-repair-5 receipt shape (no unfold_blob) must not validate."""
   155	        rec = self._good(); rec.pop("unfold_blob")
   156	        rc, out = self._run_gate(self._write(rec))
   157	        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)
   158	        self.assertIn("missing required keys", out)

   162	        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)
   163	        self.assertIn("receipt tag", out)

   174	    def test_empty_and_malformed_receipts_rejected(self):
   175	        empty = self.d / "empty.done"; empty.write_text("")
   176	        rc, out = self._run_gate(empty)

   190	class LauncherWiring(unittest.TestCase):
   191	    """The launcher must USE the gate, and must not be able to report success without a
   192	    receipt. Asserted against the shell source because executing the real launcher needs
   193	    PyROOT, 538 GB of input and Slurm."""
   194	
   195	    SH = (ND / "run_p4_unfold_std.sh").read_text()

   200	        i_gate = self.SH.index("p4_check_receipt.py")
   201	        self.assertLess(i_gate, i_skip, "the gate must run before the SKIP is announced")
   202

   204	        self.assertIn("STALE", self.SH)

   211	                        "the receipt-write failure branch must precede the DONE announcement")
